~~~python
        if abs(Dp) > L * rho:
            return None
        kp = L if (Dp > 0) == (side > 0) else -L
        if L == 0:
            return None
        eta = Dp / kp
    lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(F(s[jstar]), beta, F(hi[jstar]))
    if not (lo_ <= eta <= up_):
        return None
    law = ('lin', kp, (jstar,), c0 - kp * F(s[jstar]))
    return law, {jstar: eta}, omega

def diff_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b):
    """引理 13-B1′：常数裕度规则 |y1 - y0| > B0 + 2r，B0 = eps + b（b > 0）或 0（b = 0）。可靠；RLC 支配之。"""
    return 1 if abs(y1 - y0) > pop_null_bound(eps, b) + 2 * r else None

def avail_ok(i_in_T, s_read, r_s, eps, form_record):
    """可用性原子（D17）：i ∈ T、阈上（状态读数下界 s_read - r_s > eps）、Form 有早于 t 的记录——三者分列；
    状态读数只核阈上，不代替 Form 证据。form_record 取 True / False / None（未定）。"""
    if not i_in_T:
        return False
    if s_read - r_s <= eps:
        return None
    return form_record

def pop_null_bound(eps, b):
    """命题 13-E：H0（无一个体以 i 为支持）下单个个体有效分项之差的上界：b > 0 时 eps + b，b = 0 时 0。"""
    return (eps + b) if b > 0 else ZERO

def pop_supp_exists(ybar0, ybar1, r, eps, b):
    """命题 13-E（一）：群体「支持存在」SuppEx（∃n：i ∈ Supp_act(m, s_n)）的证书。只认证函数支持，不认证可用性或 HistCond。"""
    return 1 if abs(ybar1 - ybar0) > pop_null_bound(eps, b) + 2 * r else None

pop_exists = pop_supp_exists   # 别名（语义：支持存在）

def pop_fraction(ybar0, ybar1, r, eps, b, M1):
    """命题 13-E（二）：支持比例下界。返回 ('bound', f)、('none', 原因) 或 ('incompatible', 说明)。
    前件 M1 > B0；只在 B0 < |Δ|_low <= M1 时给非平凡下界 f = (|Δ|_low - B0)/(M1 - B0)；|Δ|_low > max(M1, B0) 时读数与申报模型不相容，不出证书。"""
    B0 = pop_null_bound(eps, b)
    d = abs(ybar1 - ybar0) - 2 * r
    if d > max(M1, B0):
        return ('incompatible', '读数下界超过申报模型可产生的最大平均差 max(M1, B0)')
    if M1 <= B0:
        return ('none', 'M1 <= B0：支持个体与非支持个体的差值界不可分，无非平凡下界')
    if d <= B0:
        return ('none', '|Δ|_low <= B0')
    return ('bound', (d - B0) / (M1 - B0))

def pop_fraction_lower(ybar0, ybar1, r, eps, b, M1):
    """数值接口：返回下界数值（无非平凡下界或不相容时返回 0）。"""
    kind, v = pop_fraction(ybar0, ybar1, r, eps, b, M1)
    return v if kind == 'bound' else ZERO

def avail_fraction_lower(mean_s, r_s, eps, hi):
    """阈上个体比例下界：个体状态在 [0, hi] 内，群体平均读数 mean_s（半径 r_s）。
    mean <= a·hi + (1 - a)·eps ⇒ a >= (mean_low - eps)/(hi - eps)。返回下界（可为 0）或 None（读数与申报不相容）。"""
    m = mean_s - r_s
    if mean_s + r_s < 0 or m > hi:
        return None
    if m <= eps:
        return ZERO
    return (m - eps) / (hi - eps)

def pop_hc_exists(f_low, a_low):
    """命题 13-E（五）：支持比例下界 f_low 与可用比例下界 a_low（可用 = i ∈ T、阈上、有 Form 记录）满足 f_low + a_low > 1 时，
    存在一个体同时支持与可用，即 P_HC^∃ = 1；否则 ⊥。"""
    if f_low is None or a_low is None:
        return None
    return 1 if f_low + a_low > 1 else None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Hoeffding 半径（有理上界）
def exp_lower(u, K=80):
    """e^u 的 Taylor 部分和（u >= 0 时为下界）。"""
    s, term = F(1), F(1)
    for k in range(1, K):
        term = term * u / k
        s += term
    return s

def ln_upper(x, den=1000):
    """有理 u，使 e^u >= x（故 u >= ln x）；精度 1/den。"""
    lo, hi = F(0), F(1)
    while exp_lower(hi) < x:
        hi *= 2
    while hi - lo > F(1, den):
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2
        if exp_lower(mid) >= x:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    return hi

def sqrt_upper(q, den=10 ** 6):
    """有理 t >= sqrt(q)，t 的分母为 den。"""
    if q == 0:
        return F(0)
    lo, hi = F(0), max(F(1), q)
    while hi - lo > F(1, den):
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2
        if mid * mid >= q:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    t = F(-((-hi.numerator * den) // hi.denominator), den)
    assert t * t >= q
    return t

def hoeffding_radius(n, tau, a):
    """n 个独立同分布、支撑于 [mu - tau, mu + tau] 的读数之均值：P(|mean - mu| >= t) <= 2 exp(-n t^2 / (2 tau^2)) <= a。"""
    u = ln_upper(F(2) / a)
    return sqrt_upper(2 * tau * tau * u / n)

def mean_dist_two_point(n, tau):
    """读数误差 e in {-tau, +tau} 各 1/2 时 n 个读数均值误差的精确分布：{误差: 概率}。"""
    out = {}
    for b in range(n + 1):
        e = tau * F(2 * b - n, n)
        out[e] = out.get(e, ZERO) + F(comb(n, b), 2 ** n)
    return out
~~~

A.2　t13\_ext.py　扩展库：Fourier–Motzkin、嵌套半径、共用通道、双线性预算与孪生、I\_full 与后继入盒的闭包保守算法、同拍门控证书、模型中介原像（5.3、5.5、5.6、5.8）

~~~python
"""《物质场具化判定式》附录程序：扩展库（精确有理运算，只用标准库）。依赖 t13_core.py。
各函数对应正文命题：
  一般工具：fm_feasible（Fourier–Motzkin 可行性与见证）
  统计与组合：nested_radius（簇内相关的嵌套 Hoeffding 半径，命题 D-2）、joint_channel_rule（共用测量通道的联合相容集，命题 D-3）
  合法域边界：b_bil_legal / twin_bil（双线性律合法域精确上确界与孪生，命题 D-5）、ifull_feasible（I_full 结构族可达判定的闭包保守算法，命题 D-6；精确算法见 t13_exact）、
          sh_feasible（后继入盒变式的闭包保守算法，命题 D-7）
  门控类同拍：gate_rules（同拍门控通道：输入读数证书与稳健越阈证书，命题 G-1—G-3）
  比较型观测族：mediated_preimage（模型中介读数的原像相容集，命题 C-3）
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_core import hat, consistent, gap, dist_sets, legal_side_interval
ZERO = F(0)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Fourier–Motzkin
def fm_feasible(cons, variables):
    """cons：[(dict 变量→系数, rhs)]，表示 Σ a·x ≤ rhs（闭约束）。返回 (可行?, 见证 dict 或 None)。"""
    stages = []
    cur = [({k: F(x) for k, x in a.items() if x != 0}, F(r)) for a, r in cons]
    for v in variables:
        pos = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) > 0]
        neg = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) < 0]
        zer = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) == 0]
        stages.append((v, pos, neg))
        new = list(zer)
        for ap, rp in pos:
            for an, rn in neg:
                cp, cn = ap[v], -an[v]
                a = {}
                for k in set(ap) | set(an):
                    if k == v:
                        continue
                    val = ap.get(k, 0) / cp + an.get(k, 0) / cn
                    if val != 0:
                        a[k] = val
                new.append((a, rp / cp + rn / cn))
        cur = new
    for a, r in cur:
        if not a and r < 0:
            return False, None
        if a:
            raise ValueError('未消去的变量')
    wit = {}
    for v, pos, neg in reversed(stages):
        ub, lb = None, None
        for a, r in pos:
            val = (r - sum(c * wit[k] for k, c in a.items() if k != v)) / a[v]
            ub = val if ub is None or val < ub else ub
        for a, r in neg:
            val = (r - sum(c * wit[k] for k, c in a.items() if k != v)) / a[v]
            lb = val if lb is None or val > lb else lb
        if lb is None and ub is None:
            wit[v] = ZERO
        elif lb is None:
            wit[v] = ub
        elif ub is None:
            wit[v] = lb
        else:
            wit[v] = (lb + ub) / 2
    return True, wit

def fm_bounds(cons, elim, target):
    """消去 elim 中各变量后，返回 target 的 (下界, 上界, 常数约束可行?)。"""
    cur = [({k: F(x) for k, x in a.items() if x != 0}, F(r)) for a, r in cons]
    for v in elim:
        pos = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) > 0]
        neg = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) < 0]
        new = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) == 0]
        for ap, rp in pos:
            for an, rn in neg:
                cp, cn = ap[v], -an[v]
                a = {k: ap.get(k, 0) / cp + an.get(k, 0) / cn for k in set(ap) | set(an) if k != v}
                a = {k: x for k, x in a.items() if x != 0}
                new.append((a, rp / cp + rn / cn))
        cur = new
    lb = ub = None
    for a, r in cur:
        c = a.get(target, 0)
        if c == 0:
            if r < 0:
                return None, None, False
        elif c > 0:
            ub = r / c if ub is None or r / c < ub else ub
        else:
            lb = r / c if lb is None or r / c > lb else lb
    if lb is not None and ub is not None and lb > ub:
        return lb, ub, False
    return lb, ub, True

def pieces_with_slope(y, r, eps):
    """相容集 I(y; r, eps) 的各段及其上 hat 的斜率（有效段 1，零段 0）。"""
    out = []
    lo, hi = y - r, y + r
    if lo < -eps:
        out.append(((lo, min(hi, -eps)), 1))
    if hi > eps:
        out.append(((max(lo, eps), hi), 1))
    if lo <= 0 <= hi:
        out.append(((-eps, eps), 0))
    return [(p, a) for p, a in out if p[0] <= p[1]]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 统计与组合
def sqrt_up(q, den=10 ** 6):
    from t13_core import sqrt_upper
    return sqrt_upper(q, den)

def nested_radius(G, n, tau_b, tau_e, a):
    """命题 D-2：G 个簇（簇偏差独立同分布、均值 0、支撑 ±tau_b），共 n 个单元（单元误差独立同分布、均值 0、支撑 ±tau_e），
    总均值 = 簇偏差均值（按簇数加权）+ 单元误差均值。各分 a/2 用 Hoeffding：t1（G 个簇）、t2（n 个单元）。返回 t1 + t2。"""
    from t13_core import ln_upper
    u = ln_upper(F(4) / a)
    t1 = sqrt_up(2 * tau_b * tau_b * u / G)
    t2 = sqrt_up(2 * tau_e * tau_e * u / n)
    return t1 + t2

def joint_channel_rule(y0, y1, r, tau_ch, eps, b):
    """命题 D-3：基线与设定后读数共用通道偏差 o（|o| ≤ tau_ch，两读数同号同值），其余误差半径 r。
    判 1 当且仅当 min_{|o|≤tau_ch} dist(I(y0 - o; r), I(y1 - o; r)) > b。dist 关于 o 分段线性，最小值在候选点上取得。"""
    cands = {tau_ch, -tau_ch}
    for y in (y0, y1):
        for sg in (r, -r):
            for v in (-eps, ZERO, eps):
                o = y + sg - v
                if -tau_ch <= o <= tau_ch:
                    cands.add(o)
    pts = sorted(cands)
    mids = [(pts[k] + pts[k + 1]) / 2 for k in range(len(pts) - 1)]
    vals = []
    for o in pts + mids:
        A, B = consistent(y0 - o, r, eps), consistent(y1 - o, r, eps)
        if A and B:                       # 空相容集表示该 o 与读数不相容，跳过
            vals.append(dist_sets(A, B))
    if not vals:
        return None, None                 # 读数与通道申报不相容：不出证书
    best = min(vals)
    return (1 if best > b else None), best

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 双线性律合法域
def bil_corner_max(s1, s2, E1, E2):
    """max |(s1+e1)(s2+e2) - s1 s2| over corners of E1 × E2（双线性函数极值在角点）。"""
    return max(abs((s1 + e1) * (s2 + e2) - s1 * s2) for e1 in E1 for e2 in E2)

def b_bil_legal(s, i, Ni, beta, hi, K, positions, dprime_max):
    """命题 D-5：带截距双线性律 H0（i ∉ Supp_act），|k| ≤ K，冻结合法域与已知基线下 |c(s~) - c(s)| 的精确上确界。
    positions：全部位；dprime_max：容许设定后 s(i) + delta' 的最大值。返回 (上确界, 达到它的读取对与角点)。"""
    E = {}
    for p in positions:
        if p in Ni:
            lo, up = legal_side_interval(s[p], beta, hi[p])
            E[p] = (lo, up)
        else:
            E[p] = (ZERO, ZERO)
    best, arg = ZERO, None
    others = [p for p in positions if p != i]
    for a_ in range(len(others)):
        for b_ in range(a_, len(others)):
            j1, j2 = others[a_], others[b_]
            if j1 == j2:
                lo, up = E[j1]
                v = max(abs((s[j1] + e) ** 2 - s[j1] ** 2) for e in (lo, up))
            else:
                v = bil_corner_max(s[j1], s[j2], E[j1], E[j2])
            if K * v > best:
                best, arg = K * v, ('pair', j1, j2)
    # 交叉情形：i 与 j2，s(j2) = 0（i ∉ Supp），响应 k·(s_i + delta')·eta_2，eta_2 ∈ [0, up]
    for j in others:
        if s[j] == 0:
            v = dprime_max * max(abs(E[j][0]), abs(E[j][1]))
            if K * v > best:
                best, arg = K * v, ('cross', i, j)
    return best, arg

def twin_bil(c0, c1, s, i, Ni, beta, hi, K, positions, dprime_max):
    """按 b_bil_legal 的达到者构造合法 H0 双线性孪生：律 b' + k s(j1) s(j2)（或交叉 k s(i) s(j)），返回 (律, eta 字典, 设定后 s(i) 值或 None)。"""
    D = c1 - c0
    if D == 0:
        return ('const', c0), {}, None
    bmax, arg = b_bil_legal(s, i, Ni, beta, hi, K, positions, dprime_max)
    if abs(D) > bmax or arg is None:
        return None
    kind, j1, j2 = arg
    E = {p: (legal_side_interval(s[p], beta, hi[p]) if p in Ni else (ZERO, ZERO)) for p in positions}
    if kind == 'pair':
        best = None
        corners = [(e1, e2) for e1 in E[j1] for e2 in E[j2]] if j1 != j2 else [(e, e) for e in E[j1]]
        for e1, e2 in corners:
            g = ((s[j1] + e1) * (s[j2] + e2) - s[j1] * s[j2]) if j1 != j2 else ((s[j1] + e1) ** 2 - s[j1] ** 2)
            if g != 0 and (best is None or abs(g) > abs(best[0])):
                best = (g, e1, e2)
        if D == 0:
            return ('const', c0), {}, None
        g, e1, e2 = best
        k = D / g
        law = ('bil', k, (j1, j2), c0 - k * s[j1] * s[j2])
        eta = {j1: e1} if j1 == j2 else {j1: e1, j2: e2}
        return law, eta, None
    # cross
    e = E[j2][1] if abs(E[j2][1]) >= abs(E[j2][0]) else E[j2][0]
    if D == 0:
        return ('const', c0), {}, None
    k = D / (dprime_max * e)
    law = ('bil', k, (i, j2), c0)     # s(j2) = 0 ⇒ 基线分项 = 截距
    return law, {j2: e}, dprime_max

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- I_full 结构族（闭包保守）
def ifull_feasible(y0, y1, r, eps, L, s_y, Ey, R, yR1, rR, S_base, s_g, need_succ=True):
    """命题 D-6：I_full 结构族的 H0 可达判定。
    m 的 H0 律只读 N_i = {y}（带截距线性，|k| ≤ L），作用对象为位 g；另一过程 R（律已知 b_R + k_R s(y)，也作用于 g）读数 yR1（设定后，半径 rR）。
    S_base：基线时 g 的一步后继中除 m 以外的部分（s_g + hat c_R(基线)，已知）；设定后同理为 s_g + hat c_R(s_y + eta)。
    变量 (c0, c1, eta)。返回 (存在孪生?, 见证)。L 为 None（未申报）时另行处理。"""
    bR, kR = R
    for P0, a0 in pieces_with_slope(y0, r, eps):
        for Pc1, a1 in pieces_with_slope(y1, r, eps):
            for PR, aR in pieces_with_slope(yR1, rR, eps):
                for sgn in (1, -1):
                    cons = []
                    cons += [({'c0': F(1)}, P0[1]), ({'c0': F(-1)}, -P0[0])]
                    cons += [({'c1': F(1)}, Pc1[1]), ({'c1': F(-1)}, -Pc1[0])]
                    cons += [({'e': F(1)}, Ey[1]), ({'e': F(-1)}, -Ey[0])]
                    # R 的设定后读数：c_R = bR + kR (s_y + e) ∈ PR
                    cons += [({'e': kR}, PR[1] - bR - kR * s_y), ({'e': -kR}, -(PR[0] - bR - kR * s_y))]
                    # eta 的符号分支
                    cons += [({'e': F(-sgn)}, ZERO)]
                    if L is not None:
                        # |c1 - c0| ≤ L·sgn·e
                        cons += [({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1), 'e': -L * sgn}, ZERO),
                                 ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1), 'e': -L * sgn}, ZERO)]
                    if need_succ:
                        # 基线后继：S_base + a0·c0 ≥ 0；设定后后继：s_g + aR·c_R(e) + a1·c1 ≥ 0
                        cons += [({'c0': F(-a0)}, S_base)]
                        cons += [({'c1': F(-a1), 'e': -aR * kR}, s_g + aR * (bR + kR * s_y))]
                    if L is not None:
                        ok, w = fm_feasible(cons, ['c1', 'c0', 'e'])
                        if ok:
                            return True, w
                        continue
                    # 未申报 L：孪生须 eta ≠ 0（任意系数），或 c1 = c0（常数律）
                    ok, w = fm_feasible(cons + [({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1)}, ZERO), ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1)}, ZERO)],
                                        ['c1', 'c0', 'e'])
                    if ok:
                        return True, w
                    lb, ub, okc = fm_bounds(cons, ['c1', 'c0'], 'e')
                    if okc:
                        top = ub if sgn > 0 else (-lb if lb is not None else None)
                        if top is None or top > 0:
                            e_val = (ub if sgn > 0 else lb)
                            if e_val is None:
                                e_val = F(sgn)
                            ok, w = fm_feasible(cons + [({'e': F(1)}, e_val), ({'e': F(-1)}, -e_val)], ['c1', 'c0', 'e'])
                            if ok:
                                return True, w
    return False, None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 后继入盒变式（I_pair，闭包保守）
def sh_feasible(y0, y1, r, eps, b, dlo, dhi, H):
    """命题 D-7：I_pair 下后继也受盒约束（tgt 位上界 H）。孪生加常数背景 K 于 tgt：两次后继 S0 + hat c0 + K、S0 + d + hat c1 + K 均在 [0, H]，
    d ∈ [dlo, dhi] 为设定在 tgt 上的状态改变（tgt ∉ {i} ∪ N_i 时 d = 0）。可行当且仅当存在 c0 ∈ I0、c1 ∈ I1、d：
    |c1 - c0| ≤ b 且 |d + hat c1 - hat c0| ≤ H。变量 (c0, c1, d)。"""
    for P0, a0 in pieces_with_slope(y0, r, eps):
        for Pc1, a1 in pieces_with_slope(y1, r, eps):
            cons = [({'c0': F(1)}, P0[1]), ({'c0': F(-1)}, -P0[0]), ({'c1': F(1)}, Pc1[1]), ({'c1': F(-1)}, -Pc1[0]),
                    ({'d': F(1)}, dhi), ({'d': F(-1)}, -dlo),
                    ({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1)}, b), ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1)}, b),
                    ({'d': F(1), 'c1': F(a1), 'c0': F(-a0)}, H), ({'d': F(-1), 'c1': F(-a1), 'c0': F(a0)}, H)]
            ok, w = fm_feasible(cons, ['c1', 'c0', 'd'])
            if ok:
                return True, w
    return False, None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 𝒦_g 同拍门控通道
def gate_input_cert(v0, v1, rv, Xi):
    """命题 G-1：门控输入 vt 的读数（半径 rv）。H0（同拍：T 不在 gr_j 中）下 |Δvt| ≤ Xi。|v1 - v0| > Xi + 2rv 判「T ∈ gr_j 且设定改变 vt」。"""
    return 1 if abs(v1 - v0) > Xi + 2 * rv else None

def robust_crossing_cert(v0, v1, rv, Xi, theta):
    """命题 G-2：稳健越阈。基线 vt0 + rv < theta 且设定后 vt1 - rv - Xi ≥ theta（或反向）⇒ 仅以 T 的设定（无副作用）即改变门控 ⇒ FD_𝒟₁(e) = 1。"""
    if v0 + rv < theta and v1 - rv - Xi >= theta:
        return 1
    if v0 - rv >= theta and v1 + rv + Xi < theta:
        return 1
    return None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 模型中介读数
def mediated_preimage(z, tau, zeta_phi, phi_pieces):
    """命题 C-3：读数 z = phi(theta) + e，|e| ≤ tau，申报模型与真映射之差 ≤ zeta_phi。phi 为分段线性单调不减（列表 [(x0, x1, y0, y1)]）。
    返回 theta 的相容集 phi^{-1}([z - tau - zeta_phi, z + tau + zeta_phi])（闭区间列表）。"""
    lo, hi = z - tau - zeta_phi, z + tau + zeta_phi
    out = []
    for x0, x1, y0, y1 in phi_pieces:
        a, b = max(lo, min(y0, y1)), min(hi, max(y0, y1))
        if a > b:
            continue
        if y1 == y0:
            out.append((x0, x1))
        else:
            ta, tb = x0 + (a - y0) * (x1 - x0) / (y1 - y0), x0 + (b - y0) * (x1 - x0) / (y1 - y0)
            out.append((min(ta, tb), max(ta, tb)))
    return out
~~~

A.3　t13\_exact.py　真相容集、带严格不等式的 Fourier–Motzkin、I\_full 结构族与后继入盒的精确求解及回代、被测位可用域的双线性预算（5.3）

~~~python
"""《物质场具化判定式》附录程序：真相容集与精确求解（精确有理运算，只用标准库）。
t13_ext.py 的 ifull_feasible、sh_feasible 用相容集闭包，只作「闭包保守算法」（只用于保守判 1，不签精确性）。
本文件含：
  true_pieces        真相容集 J(y; r, eps) = {c : hat(c) ∈ [y - r, y + r]}，活动段在 ±eps 处为开端点，零段闭
  fm_strict          带严格不等式的 Fourier–Motzkin 可行性判定与可回代见证
  ifull_exact        I_full 结构族（其余过程仿射）的精确 H0 可达判定，返回真见证
  verify_ifull       以真实 hat 回代全部观测与后继约束，核验见证
  sh_exact / verify_sh  后继入盒变式（tgt ∉ N_i 子类）的精确判定与回代
  b_bil_avail        D-5 的双线性预算，只在被测位可用（s_i > eps ≥ 0）时给出
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_core import hat, legal_side_interval
from t13_ext import b_bil_legal
ZERO = F(0)

def true_pieces(y, r, eps):
    """真相容集的各段：(lo, hi, lo_strict, hi_strict, slope)。活动段 slope = 1（hat(c) = c），零段 slope = 0。"""
    out = []
    lo, hi = y - r, y + r
    if lo < -eps:
        up = min(hi, -eps)
        out.append((lo, up, False, hi >= -eps, 1))
    if hi > eps:
        dn = max(lo, eps)
        out.append((dn, hi, lo <= eps, False, 1))
    if lo <= 0 <= hi:
        out.append((-eps, eps, False, False, 0))
    return out

def in_true(c, y, r, eps):
    return y - r <= hat(c, eps) <= y + r

def fm_strict(cons, variables):
    """cons：[(dict 变量→系数, rhs, strict)]，表示 Σ a·x < rhs（strict）或 ≤ rhs。返回 (可行?, 见证)。
    带严格不等式的 Fourier–Motzkin 消去对投影是精确的；回代时在开区间内取中点，故见证满足全部严格约束。"""
    cur = [({k: F(x) for k, x in a.items() if x != 0}, F(r), bool(st)) for a, r, st in cons]
    stages = []
    for v in variables:
        pos = [c for c in cur if c[0].get(v, 0) > 0]
        neg = [c for c in cur if c[0].get(v, 0) < 0]
        new = [c for c in cur if c[0].get(v, 0) == 0]
        stages.append((v, pos, neg))
        for ap, rp, sp in pos:
            for an, rn, sn in neg:
                cp, cn = ap[v], -an[v]
                a = {k: ap.get(k, 0) / cp + an.get(k, 0) / cn for k in set(ap) | set(an) if k != v}
                a = {k: x for k, x in a.items() if x != 0}
                new.append((a, rp / cp + rn / cn, sp or sn))
        cur = new
    for a, r, st in cur:
        if a:
            raise ValueError('未消去的变量')
        if (st and not (r > 0)) or (not st and r < 0):
            return False, None
    wit = {}
    for v, pos, neg in reversed(stages):
        ups = [((r - sum(c * wit[k] for k, c in a.items() if k != v)) / a[v], st) for a, r, st in pos]
        dns = [((r - sum(c * wit[k] for k, c in a.items() if k != v)) / a[v], st) for a, r, st in neg]
        ub = min(x for x, _ in ups) if ups else None
        lb = max(x for x, _ in dns) if dns else None
        ubs = any(st for x, st in ups if x == ub)
        lbs = any(st for x, st in dns if x == lb)
        if lb is None and ub is None:
            x = ZERO
        elif lb is None:
            x = ub - 1 if ubs else ub
        elif ub is None:
            x = lb + 1 if lbs else lb
        elif lb < ub:
            x = (lb + ub) / 2
        else:
            if lbs or ubs:
                raise AssertionError('回代失败：投影可行而端点严格')
            x = lb
        wit[v] = x
    return True, wit

def _piece_cons(var, p):
    lo, hi, ls, hs, _a = p
    return [({var: F(1)}, hi, hs), ({var: F(-1)}, -lo, ls)]

def ifull_exact(y0, y1, r, eps, L, s_y, Ey, R, yR1, rR, S_base, s_g):
    """命题 D-6 结构族：m 的 H0 律只读 N_i = {y}（带截距线性，|k| ≤ L，L 为 None 表示未申报）；
    其余过程 R（仿射律 b_R + k_R s(y)）与 m 同作用于 g；R 的设定后读数 yR1（半径 rR）；后继须非负。
    变量 (c0, c1, e)。用真相容集与严格不等式；返回 (存在孪生?, 见证)。"""
    bR, kR = R
    for Pc0 in true_pieces(y0, r, eps):
        for Pc1 in true_pieces(y1, r, eps):
            for Pcr in true_pieces(yR1, rR, eps):
                a0, a1, aR = Pc0[4], Pc1[4], Pcr[4]
                base = _piece_cons('c0', Pc0) + _piece_cons('c1', Pc1)
                base += [({'e': F(1)}, Ey[1], False), ({'e': F(-1)}, -Ey[0], False)]
                lo, hi, ls, hs, _ = Pcr       # c_R(e) = bR + kR (s_y + e) 落在该段
                base += [({'e': kR}, hi - bR - kR * s_y, hs), ({'e': -kR}, -(lo - bR - kR * s_y), ls)]
                base += [({'c0': F(-a0)}, S_base, False)]
                base += [({'c1': F(-a1), 'e': -aR * kR}, s_g + aR * (bR + kR * s_y), False)]
                branches = []
                if L is not None:
                    for sg in (1, -1):
                        branches.append(base + [({'e': F(-sg)}, ZERO, False),
                                                ({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1), 'e': -L * sg}, ZERO, False),
                                                ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1), 'e': -L * sg}, ZERO, False)])
                else:
                    branches.append(base + [({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1)}, ZERO, False), ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1)}, ZERO, False)])
                    for sg in (1, -1):
                        branches.append(base + [({'e': F(-sg)}, ZERO, True)])     # sg·e > 0，系数任意
                for cons in branches:
                    ok, w = fm_strict(cons, ['c1', 'c0', 'e'])
                    if ok:
                        return True, w
    return False, None

def verify_ifull(w, y0, y1, r, eps, L, s_y, Ey, R, yR1, rR, S_base, s_g):
    """由见证构造孪生 m' = b' + k' s(y)，以真实 hat 回代全部观测与后继约束。"""
    c0, c1, e = w['c0'], w['c1'], w['e']
    if e != 0:
        kp = (c1 - c0) / e
    else:
        if c1 != c0:
            return False
        kp = ZERO
    if L is not None and abs(kp) > L:
        return False
    if not (Ey[0] <= e <= Ey[1]):
        return False
    t0 = c0                     # m'(s_y) = b' + k' s_y = c0
    t1 = c0 + kp * e            # = c1
    cR1 = hat(R[0] + R[1] * (s_y + e), eps)
    return (in_true(t0, y0, r, eps) and in_true(t1, y1, r, eps) and yR1 - rR <= cR1 <= yR1 + rR
            and S_base + hat(t0, eps) >= 0 and s_g + cR1 + hat(t1, eps) >= 0)

def sh_exact(y0, y1, r, eps, b, dlo, dhi, H):
    """命题 D-7（tgt ∉ N_i 子类）：存在 c0 ∈ J0、c1 ∈ J1、d ∈ [dlo, dhi]，|c1 - c0| ≤ b，|d + hat c1 - hat c0| ≤ H。"""
    for Pc0 in true_pieces(y0, r, eps):
        for Pc1 in true_pieces(y1, r, eps):
            a0, a1 = Pc0[4], Pc1[4]
            cons = _piece_cons('c0', Pc0) + _piece_cons('c1', Pc1)
            cons += [({'d': F(1)}, dhi, False), ({'d': F(-1)}, -dlo, False),
                     ({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1)}, b, False), ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1)}, b, False),
                     ({'d': F(1), 'c1': F(a1), 'c0': F(-a0)}, H, False), ({'d': F(-1), 'c1': F(-a1), 'c0': F(a0)}, H, False)]
            ok, w = fm_strict(cons, ['c1', 'c0', 'd'])
            if ok:
                return True, w
    return False, None

def verify_sh(w, y0, y1, r, eps, b, dlo, dhi, H):
    c0, c1, d = w['c0'], w['c1'], w['d']
    return (in_true(c0, y0, r, eps) and in_true(c1, y1, r, eps) and abs(c1 - c0) <= b and dlo <= d <= dhi
            and abs(d + hat(c1, eps) - hat(c0, eps)) <= H)

def b_bil_avail(s, i, Ni, beta, hi, K, positions, dprime_max, eps):
    """命题 D-5：只在被测位可用（s_i > eps ≥ 0）时给出双线性预算；否则返回 None（不在定理域内）。
    在此域内读取对 (i, i) 的 H0 只能 k = 0（D15：s_i ≠ 0 时 i ∈ Supp），情形穷尽。"""
    if not (s[i] > eps >= 0):
        return None
    return b_bil_legal(s, i, Ni, beta, hi, K, positions, dprime_max)[0]
~~~

A.4　t13\_gate.py　多拍门控库：𝒦\_g、第三因子、𝒦\_PA^δ、𝒦\_PA^≺ 的运行与事件、R1′ 核对、同时方程枚举、传播上界、分项差界、M-1、读数固定阈、M-2 孪生（3.6、5.7、8.1）

~~~python
"""《物质场具化判定式》附录程序：多拍门控通道。精确有理运算，只用标准库。
对象（正文 3.6、5.7）：
  mode = 'g'      《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 定义 1—2 的 𝒦_g（gate_of[j] = j），及第三因子变体（gate_of[j] ≠ j，𝒦_PA^3）；
  mode = 'delay'  𝒦_PA^δ：门控单元的发放以权重 U[k][j] 延迟一拍输入单元 j 的积分（状态加辅助位 h = 上一拍发放向量）；
  mode = 'dag'    𝒦_PA^≺：同拍发放输入，U 的支撑图须为有向无环图，按申报拓扑次序 order 逐单元积分、门控。
  同拍循环发放输入（同时方程）不属本篇工作域：simultaneous_solutions 只用于展示其解可不唯一（正文 3.6 例 3.6-1）。
函数：
  validate          类成员检查（参数域、DAG 次序）
  step / run        一拍与多拍运行（返回逐拍拍前状态、门控、门控输入、全部事件 (发生, 分项)）
  r1_check          引理 R1′（K_t 闭合：拍前值 + 指向该结点的分项和 = 拍后值）
  simultaneous_solutions  同拍方程组 g_j = [base_j + Σ_k U[k][j] g_k ≥ θ_j] 的全部解（枚举）
  prop_bounds       命题 M-1 的传播递推（权重差系数 1 − ρ·[plastic]；delay 含 h 项）
  event_bound       各事件种类在拍 t_e 的分项差上界 B_e（含 RESET 等全部种类）
  m1_certificate    多拍稳健证书 M-1（发生型规则只用于发生由门控、字母、开关决定的种类；CLIP 与受 h 副作用的 FIN 用分项规则）
  beta_star         单位副作用剖面下裕度条件可容许的最大缩放因子（干预界）
  m2_twin           命题 M-2：全局固定 pat_B 的 H0 孪生构造
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product
ZERO = F(0)
KINDS_W = ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP', 'SYN')

def clip(a, lo, hi):
    return min(hi, max(lo, a))

def _dx(P, i):
    d = P['dx']
    return d[i] if isinstance(d, list) else d

def _dy(P, j):
    d = P['dy']
    return d[j] if isinstance(d, list) else d

def validate(P, nin, nout):
    """类成员检查。返回 (True, '') 或 (False, 原因)。"""
    for j in range(nout):
        if not (0 <= P['lam'][j] <= 1):
            return False, 'lam'
        if not (0 <= _dy(P, j) <= 1):
            return False, 'dy'
    for i in range(nin):
        if not (0 <= _dx(P, i) <= 1):
            return False, 'dx'
    if not (0 <= P['rho'] <= 1) or P['Ap'] < 0 or P['Am'] < 0:
        return False, 'rho/A'
    mode = P.get('mode', 'g')
    U = P.get('U')
    if mode == 'g':
        if U is not None and any(U[k][j] != 0 for k in range(nout) for j in range(nout)):
            return False, 'U in K_g'
    elif mode == 'dag':
        pos = {j: n for n, j in enumerate(P['order'])}
        if sorted(P['order']) != list(range(nout)):
            return False, 'order'
        for k in range(nout):
            for j in range(nout):
                if U[k][j] != 0 and not pos[k] < pos[j]:
                    return False, 'not DAG under order'
    elif mode != 'delay':
        return False, 'mode'
    return True, ''

def _base(P, st, s, I):
    w, v, x, y, h = st
    nin, nout = len(x), len(v)
    return [P['lam'][j] * v[j] + sum((w[i][j] for i in range(nin) if s[i]), ZERO) + I[j] for j in range(nout)]

def step(P, st, s, I):
    """st = (w, v, x, y, h)。返回 (新态, g, vt, ev)；ev[事件] = (发生, 分项)，不发生时分项记 0。"""
    w, v, x, y, h = st
    nin, nout = len(x), len(v)
    mode = P.get('mode', 'g')
    U = P.get('U') or [[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nout)]
    base = _base(P, st, s, I)
    if mode == 'g':
        vt = base[:]
        g = [vt[j] >= P['theta'][j] for j in range(nout)]
    elif mode == 'delay':
        vt = [base[j] + sum((U[k][j] for k in range(nout) if h[k]), ZERO) for j in range(nout)]
        g = [vt[j] >= P['theta'][j] for j in range(nout)]
    else:  # dag：按申报拓扑次序
        vt = [None] * nout
        g = [None] * nout
        for j in P['order']:
            vt[j] = base[j] + sum((U[k][j] for k in range(nout) if U[k][j] != 0 and g[k]), ZERO)
            g[j] = vt[j] >= P['theta'][j]
    v2 = [P['vres'][j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(nout)]
    x2 = [_dx(P, i) * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(nin)]
    y2 = [_dy(P, j) * y[j] + (1 if g[j] else 0) for j in range(nout)]
    h2 = [1 if g[j] else 0 for j in range(nout)]
    w2 = [[None] * nout for _ in range(nin)]
    ev = {}
    pl, bp = P['plastic'], P['bap']
    for i in range(nin):
        for j in range(nout):
            G = g[P['gate_of'][j]]
            occp = bool(pl and bp and G)
            occm = bool(pl and bp and s[i])
            pp = P['Ap'] * x2[i] if occp else ZERO
            pm = -P['Am'] * y2[P['gate_of'][j]] if occm else ZERO
            occr = bool(pl and P['rho'] != 0)
            pr = -P['rho'] * (w[i][j] - P['b'][i][j]) if pl else ZERO
            S = w[i][j] + pp + pm + pr
            w2[i][j] = clip(S, P['lo'], P['hi'])
            cc = w2[i][j] - S
            ev[('PLUS', i, j)] = (occp, pp)
            ev[('MINUS', i, j)] = (occm, pm)
            ev[('RELAX', i, j)] = (occr, pr)
            ev[('CLIP', i, j)] = (cc != 0, cc)
            ev[('SYN', i, j)] = (bool(s[i]), w[i][j] if s[i] else ZERO)
    for i in range(nin):
        ev[('XIN', i)] = (bool(s[i]), F(1) if s[i] else ZERO)
        ev[('XDEC', i)] = (True, (_dx(P, i) - 1) * x[i])
    for j in range(nout):
        ev[('LEAK', j)] = (True, (P['lam'][j] - 1) * v[j])
        ev[('INJ', j)] = (I[j] != 0, I[j])
        ev[('RESET', j)] = (g[j], P['vres'][j] - vt[j] if g[j] else ZERO)
        ev[('YIN', j)] = (g[j], F(1) if g[j] else ZERO)
        ev[('YDEC', j)] = (True, (_dy(P, j) - 1) * y[j])
        for k in range(nout):
            if U[k][j] != 0 and mode != 'g':
                on = bool(h[k]) if mode == 'delay' else bool(g[k])
                ev[('FIN', k, j)] = (on, U[k][j] if on else ZERO)
        if mode == 'delay':
            ev[('HCOPY', j)] = (True, F(h2[j] - h[j]))
    return (w2, v2, x2, y2, h2), g, vt, ev

def copy_state(st):
    w, v, x, y, h = st
    return ([row[:] for row in w], v[:], x[:], y[:], h[:])

def run(P, st0, seq, u=None, setting=None, side=None):
    """seq：[(s, I)]。拍 u 开始前把 setting（{(i, j): 值}）与 side（{('w',i,j)|('v',j)|('x',i)|('y',j): 增量；('h',k): 翻转}）施于状态。
    返回逐拍记录 dict(pre=拍前态, g, vt, ev)。"""
    st = copy_state(st0)
    out = []
    for t, (s, I) in enumerate(seq):
        if u is not None and t == u:
            w, v, x, y, h = copy_state(st)
            for (i, j), val in (setting or {}).items():
                w[i][j] = val
            for key, d in (side or {}).items():
                if key[0] == 'w':
                    w[key[1]][key[2]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'v':
                    v[key[1]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'x':
                    x[key[1]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'y':
                    y[key[1]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'h':
                    if d:
                        h[key[1]] = 1 - h[key[1]]
            st = (w, v, x, y, h)
        pre = copy_state(st)
        st, g, vt, ev = step(P, st, s, I)
        out.append({'pre': pre, 'g': g, 'vt': vt, 'ev': ev, 'post': copy_state(st)})
    return out

def r1_check(P, rec):
    """引理 R1′：对一拍记录逐结点核对 拍前值 + Σ 分项 = 拍后值。返回不一致数。"""
    w, v, x, y, h = rec['pre']
    w2, v2, x2, y2, h2 = rec['post']
    ev = rec['ev']
    nin, nout = len(x), len(v)
    bad = 0
    for i in range(nin):
        for j in range(nout):
            tot = sum(ev[(k, i, j)][1] for k in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP'))
            bad += (w[i][j] + tot != w2[i][j])
        bad += (x[i] + ev[('XDEC', i)][1] + ev[('XIN', i)][1] != x2[i])
    for j in range(nout):
        tot = ev[('LEAK', j)][1] + ev[('INJ', j)][1] + ev[('RESET', j)][1]
        tot += sum(ev[('SYN', i, j)][1] for i in range(nin))
        tot += sum(c for (key, (o, c)) in ev.items() if key[0] == 'FIN' and key[2] == j)
        bad += (v[j] + tot != v2[j])
        bad += (y[j] + ev[('YDEC', j)][1] + ev[('YIN', j)][1] != y2[j])
        if ('HCOPY', j) in ev:
            bad += (h[j] + ev[('HCOPY', j)][1] != h2[j])
    return bad

def simultaneous_solutions(P, st, s, I):
    """同拍同时方程组 g_j = [base_j + Σ_k U[k][j]·g_k ≥ θ_j] 的全部解（2^n 枚举）。"""
    nout = len(st[1])
    U = P['U']
    base = _base(P, st, s, I)
    sols = []
    for g in product((0, 1), repeat=nout):
        if all((base[j] + sum((U[k][j] for k in range(nout) if g[k]), ZERO) >= P['theta'][j]) == bool(g[j]) for j in range(nout)):
            sols.append(g)
    return sols

def prop_bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0):
    """命题 M-1 的传播递推。D0 = (Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh)：拍 u 开始时副作用的逐结点上界（Dh 取 0/1，只用于 delay）。
    以设定实验读得的门控模式 g_obs 逐拍递推，只用申报参数：
      Dvt_j(t) = λ_j Dv_j + Σ_{i: s_i=1} Dw_ij + [delay] Σ_k |U_kj| Dh_k
      Dv_j(t+1) = 0（g_j = 1）或 Dvt_j(t)；Dx_i(t+1) = d_x,i Dx_i；Dy_j(t+1) = d_y,j Dy_j；Dh(t+1) = 0
      Dw_ij(t+1) = (1 − ρ·[plastic]) Dw_ij + A₊ d_x,i Dx_i·[plastic ∧ bap ∧ g_{gate(j)}] + A₋ d_y,gate(j) Dy_gate(j)·[plastic ∧ bap ∧ s_i]
    返回 (Dvt[t], Dstate[t])，Dstate[t] 为拍 t 拍前态的界，t = u..te+1（Dstate[te+1].Dw 即拍 te 夹前和 S 的差界）。"""
    Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = [row[:] for row in D0[0]], D0[1][:], D0[2][:], D0[3][:], D0[4][:]
    nin, nout = len(Dx), len(Dv)
    mode = P.get('mode', 'g')
    U = P.get('U') or [[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nout)]
    Dvt_all, Dst = {}, {}
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        Dst[t] = (Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh)
        s, I = seq[t]
        g = g_obs[t]
        Dvt = [P['lam'][j] * Dv[j] + sum((Dw[i][j] for i in range(nin) if s[i]), ZERO) for j in range(nout)]
        if mode == 'delay':
            Dvt = [Dvt[j] + sum((abs(U[k][j]) * Dh[k] for k in range(nout)), ZERO) for j in range(nout)]
        Dvt_all[t] = Dvt
        Dv2 = [ZERO if g[j] else Dvt[j] for j in range(nout)]
        Dx2 = [_dx(P, i) * Dx[i] for i in range(nin)]
        Dy2 = [_dy(P, j) * Dy[j] for j in range(nout)]
        coef = (1 - P['rho']) if P['plastic'] else F(1)
        Dw2 = [[None] * nout for _ in range(nin)]
        for i in range(nin):
            for j in range(nout):
                gj = P['gate_of'][j]
                a = coef * Dw[i][j]
                if P['plastic'] and P['bap'] and g[gj]:
                    a += P['Ap'] * Dx2[i]
                if P['plastic'] and P['bap'] and s[i]:
                    a += P['Am'] * Dy2[gj]
                Dw2[i][j] = a
        Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = Dw2, Dv2, Dx2, Dy2, [0] * nout
    Dst[te + 1] = (Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh)
    return Dvt_all, Dst

def prop_bounds_no_switch(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0):
    """仅供对照：缺 plastic 开关的错误递推（权重差系数无条件取 1 − ρ）。不用于任何证书。"""
    Pn = dict(P)
    Pn['plastic'] = True
    if not P['plastic']:
        Pn['Ap'] = ZERO
        Pn['Am'] = ZERO
    return prop_bounds(Pn, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0)

def event_bound(P, e, te, Dvt_all, Dst):
    """拍 te 事件 e 的分项差上界 B_e（只以 T 设定的运行与设定实验之间），前提为 [u, te] 门控模式相同。"""
    Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = Dst[te]
    kind = e[0]
    if kind in ('YIN', 'XIN', 'INJ'):
        return ZERO
    if kind == 'RESET':
        return Dvt_all[te][e[1]]
    if kind == 'LEAK':
        return (1 - P['lam'][e[1]]) * Dv[e[1]]
    if kind == 'SYN':
        return Dw[e[1]][e[2]]
    if kind == 'FIN':
        return abs(P['U'][e[1]][e[2]]) * Dh[e[1]] if P.get('mode') == 'delay' else ZERO
    if kind == 'HCOPY':
        return F(Dh[e[1]])
    if kind == 'XDEC':
        return (1 - _dx(P, e[1])) * Dx[e[1]]
    if kind == 'YDEC':
        return (1 - _dy(P, e[1])) * Dy[e[1]]
    if kind == 'PLUS':
        return P['Ap'] * _dx(P, e[1]) * Dx[e[1]]
    if kind == 'MINUS':
        gj = P['gate_of'][e[2]]
        return P['Am'] * _dy(P, gj) * Dy[gj]
    if kind == 'RELAX':
        return P['rho'] * Dw[e[1]][e[2]]
    if kind == 'CLIP':
        return Dst[te + 1][0][e[1]][e[2]]
    raise ValueError(kind)

def occurrence_fixed(P, e, te, Dst):
    """事件发生是否由门控、字母、开关决定（于是 [u, te] 门控模式相同 ⇒ 两运行发生相同）。"""
    if e[0] == 'CLIP':
        return False
    if e[0] == 'FIN' and P.get('mode') == 'delay' and Dst[te][4][e[1]]:
        return False
    return True

def margins_ok(P, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dvt_all):
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        for j, zz in enumerate(z_obs[t]):
            if g_obs[t][j]:
                if not (zz - rv - Dvt_all[t][j] >= P['theta'][j]):
                    return False
            else:
                if not (zz + rv + Dvt_all[t][j] < P['theta'][j]):
                    return False
    return True

def m1_certificate(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, D0, e, ev_obs, ev_act, r=ZERO, bounds=prop_bounds):
    """命题 M-1。ev_obs、ev_act = (发生, 分项读数)，发生精确、分项读数半径 r（不发生时分项记 0）。
    裕度全部成立后：发生由门控决定的种类，发生不同 ⇒ 1；两次都发生且 |c_obs − c_act| > B_e + 2r ⇒ 1；
    CLIP 与 h 受副作用的 FIN（发生不由门控决定）：|c_obs − c_act| > B_e + 2r ⇒ 1（不发生记分项 0）。
    返回 (判词 1 或 None, 裕度是否成立)。"""
    Dvt_all, Dst = bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0)
    if not margins_ok(P, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dvt_all):
        return None, False
    B = event_bound(P, e, te, Dvt_all, Dst)
    (oo, co), (oa, ca) = ev_obs, ev_act
    if occurrence_fixed(P, e, te, Dst):
        if oo != oa:
            return 1, True
        if oo and oa and abs(co - ca) > B + 2 * r:
            return 1, True
        return None, True
    if abs(co - ca) > B + 2 * r:
        return 1, True
    return None, True

def beta_star(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dhat):
    """单位副作用剖面 Dhat（Dh 须为 0）下，D = κ·Dhat 时裕度条件成立的 κ 的上确界。返回 (κ*, 是否严格)：
    κ < κ*（严格）或 κ ≤ κ*（非严格）时裕度成立；κ* = None 表示对一切 κ ≥ 0 成立；κ* < 0 表示读数本身不过裕度。"""
    assert not any(Dhat[4])
    Dvt_all, _ = prop_bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, Dhat)
    best, strict = None, False
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        for j, zz in enumerate(z_obs[t]):
            d = Dvt_all[t][j]
            if g_obs[t][j]:
                room = zz - rv - P['theta'][j]
                if d == 0:
                    if room < 0:
                        return F(-1), False
                    continue
                k, st_ = room / d, False
            else:
                room = P['theta'][j] - zz - rv
                if d == 0:
                    if room <= 0:
                        return F(-1), False
                    continue
                k, st_ = room / d, True
            if best is None or k < best or (k == best and st_):
                best, strict = k, st_
    return best, strict

def changed(ev1, ev2):
    (o1, c1), (o2, c2) = ev1, ev2
    return o1 != o2 or (o1 and o2 and c1 != c2)

# ───────────────────────── 命题 M-2：全局固定 pat_B 的孪生 ─────────────────────────
def m2_twin(P, st0, labels, patA, I_A, u, te, Tin, beta_v, ga_base, ga_set):
    """单输出 𝒦_g（mode 'g'）。冻结信息：标签序列 labels、两运行的发放读数 ga_base、ga_set（[0, te]）、T 的申报身份。
    Tin：T 对应的入口单元集合。构造：
      pat_B(a) := pat_A(a) 把 Tin 分量置 0（对每一标签一次定义，全程使用）；
      注入 I_B(t) 逐拍选取：t < u 时使 B 的发放等于 A 的前缀发放；u ≤ t < te 时使两运行都不发放；
      t = te 时使两运行的门控输入落在 θ 两侧，方向与 A 相同（副作用 ±beta_v 加于 v）。
    返回 dict(patB, I_B, side, base, set, used)；used[t] = 该拍实际使用的 (标签, 模式)，供逐拍核查映射一致。"""
    theta = P['theta'][0]
    patB = {a: tuple(0 if i in Tin else bit for i, bit in enumerate(p)) for a, p in patA.items()}
    flip_up = (not ga_base[te]) and ga_set[te]
    side = {('v', 0): beta_v if flip_up else -beta_v}
    I_B = []
    st = copy_state(st0)
    used = []
    # 前缀 t < u：两运行相同，逐拍选注入使门控 = A 的前缀发放
    for t in range(u):
        s = list(patB[labels[t]])
        used.append((labels[t], tuple(s)))
        b = _base(P, st, s, [ZERO])[0]
        I = theta - b if ga_base[t] else theta - b - 1
        I_B.append([I])
        st, g, vt, ev = step(P, st, s, [I])
    # u..te：两运行分开推进（同一注入）
    stb = copy_state(st)
    w, v, x, y, h = copy_state(st)
    v[0] += side[('v', 0)]
    sts = (w, v, x, y, h)
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        s = list(patB[labels[t]])
        used.append((labels[t], tuple(s)))
        bb = _base(P, stb, s, [ZERO])[0]
        bs = _base(P, sts, s, [ZERO])[0]
        if t < te:
            I = theta - 1 - max(bb, bs)
        else:
            D = bs - bb
            if D == 0:
                return None
            I = (theta - D / 2 - bb) if D > 0 else (theta + (-D) / 2 - bb)
            # D > 0：基线 vt = θ − D/2（不发放），设定 vt = θ + D/2（发放）；D < 0 反向
        I_B.append([I])
        stb, _, _, _ = step(P, stb, s, [I])
        sts, _, _, _ = step(P, sts, s, [I])
    seqB = [(list(patB[labels[t]]), I_B[t]) for t in range(te + 1)]
    return {'patB': patB, 'I_B': I_B, 'seqB': seqB, 'side': side, 'used': used}
~~~

A.5　t13\_f1.py　命题 F-1 的稳健越阈条件、逐事件申报、读数固定阈与事前设计阈（8.1）

~~~python
"""《物质场具化判定式》附录程序：命题 F-1 的越阈条件、逐事件申报与干预界。精确有理运算，只用标准库。
在 t13_gate 之上增加：
  f1_cross          F-1（五）DAN 稳健越阈条件（等号归发放一侧，g = [vt ≥ θ]）
  f1_event_change   F-1（六）DAN 在 t₂ 翻转时各目标事件在 t₂ 是否改变的申报（YIN_D、RESET_D、PLUS_iM、MINUS_iM）
  kappa_read        读数固定时的可接受 κ 阈（与 t13_gate.beta_star 相同，逐分支返回）
  kappa_design      事前设计阈：读数未知，以模型预测门控输入（误差 ζ）给出保证裕度成立的 κ 充分阈
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_gate import prop_bounds, beta_star
ZERO = F(0)

def f1_cross(theta, z_act, rv, shift):
    """baseline 读数 z_act（半径 rv）、模型给出的 DAN 门控输入移动 shift = U_MD·Δg_M（精确申报）。
    返回 'up'（基线不发放、只设 T 的运行发放）、'down'（反向）或 None（未保证越阈）。
    up：z_act + rv < θ 且 z_act − rv + shift ≥ θ；down：z_act − rv ≥ θ 且 z_act + rv + shift < θ。"""
    if z_act + rv < theta and z_act - rv + shift >= theta:
        return 'up'
    if z_act - rv >= theta and z_act + rv + shift < theta:
        return 'down'
    return None

def f1_event_change(P, kind, i, s_t2):
    """DAN（门控单元 D）在 t₂ 翻转、t₂ 之前 τ 与基线 DAN 相同时，t₂ 处事件是否改变（模型层）。
    YIN_D、RESET_D：发生改变；PLUS_iM：发生改变 ⟺ plastic ∧ bap；MINUS_iM：发生两运行相同（plastic ∧ bap ∧ s_i），
    分项差 −A₋·Δg_D ⟺ 改变当且仅当 plastic ∧ bap ∧ s_i(t₂) ∧ A₋ ≠ 0。其余拍不作申报。"""
    pb = bool(P['plastic'] and P['bap'])
    if kind in ('YIN', 'RESET'):
        return True
    if kind == 'PLUS':
        return pb
    if kind == 'MINUS':
        return pb and bool(s_t2[i]) and P['Am'] != 0
    raise ValueError(kind)

def kappa_read(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dhat):
    """读数固定时的可接受 κ 阈（逐 (t, j) 分支）：
      D̂_vt = 0：发放侧 m ≥ 0、不发放侧 m > 0 时该约束对一切 κ 成立，否则无 κ 可接受；
      D̂_vt > 0：发放侧 κ ≤ m/D̂，不发放侧 κ < m/D̂；m 为负（或不发放侧 m = 0）时无 κ ≥ 0 可接受；
      m = z − r_v − θ（发放）或 θ − z − r_v（不发放）。全部 D̂_vt = 0 且都满足时阈为 +∞。
    返回 (状态, κ*, 严格)：状态 'all'（一切 κ ≥ 0）、'none'（无 κ ≥ 0）、'bound'（κ ≤ κ* 或 κ < κ*）。"""
    k, strict = beta_star(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dhat)
    if k is None:
        return 'all', None, False
    if k < 0 or (k == 0 and strict):
        return 'none', k, strict
    return 'bound', k, strict

def kappa_design(P, seq, u, te, vt_pred, zeta, rv, g_pred, Dhat):
    """事前设计阈（读数未知）。vt_pred[t][j]：申报模型对只设 T 的运行门控输入的预测（|误差| ≤ zeta），g_pred 其门控。
    充分条件：发放侧 vt_pred − ζ − 2r_v − 2κ D̂_vt ≥ θ，不发放侧 vt_pred + ζ + 2r_v + 2κ D̂_vt < θ。
    （设定实验的 vt 与只设 T 的运行相差 ≤ κD̂_vt，读数再偏 r_v，裕度检验又减 r_v 与 κD̂_vt。）返回同 kappa_read。"""
    Dvt_all, _ = prop_bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_pred, Dhat)
    best, strict = None, False
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        for j in range(len(vt_pred[t])):
            d = 2 * Dvt_all[t][j]
            if g_pred[t][j]:
                room, st_ = vt_pred[t][j] - zeta - 2 * rv - P['theta'][j], False
                if not vt_pred[t][j] >= P['theta'][j]:
                    return 'none', F(-1), False
            else:
                room, st_ = P['theta'][j] - vt_pred[t][j] - zeta - 2 * rv, True
                if not vt_pred[t][j] < P['theta'][j]:
                    return 'none', F(-1), False
            if d == 0:
                if room < 0 or (st_ and room == 0):
                    return 'none', F(-1), False
                continue
            k = room / d
            if best is None or k < best or (k == best and st_):
                best, strict = k, st_
    if best is None:
        return 'all', None, False
    if best < 0 or (best == 0 and strict):
        return 'none', best, strict
    return 'bound', best, strict
~~~

A.6　run\_minimal.py　运行入口：最小实例 χ\_E（7.5）

~~~python
"""《物质场具化判定式》附录程序：最小实例 chi_E（正文 7.5）。
模型结论（A 栏），不是任何物质宿载的认证。chi_E 的结构取大肠杆菌趋化的抽象形态，参数为构造值。
申报要点：
  冻结状态盒：各位合法域 [0, hi]，缺省 hi = 2；副作用只取合法区间内的值，H0 预算取合法半径 rho（b_legal = L·rho + zeta_b）。
  T 与形成记录：T = {x}；拍 t-1 有界外入口脉冲 +1 写入 x（记录起点 x = 0 <= eps，ENT 平凡；REC 覆盖 t-1 至 t），
  由此 Form(x) 在 t-1 有记录，x 在 t 可用——这是模型内由合法历史产生的；读回物质宿载时为条件申报。
  群体：只签「支持存在」与（M1 > B0 时的）支持比例；与可用比例相交才签 P_HC^∃。
"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))   # 使 python3 -I 亦可运行
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product
from math import comb
from t13_core import (value, hat, supp_act, consistent, dist_sets, act_rule, ch3_act, ch3_R, rlc_rule, rlc_star,
                      pop_supp_exists, pop_fraction, pop_null_bound, avail_fraction_lower, pop_hc_exists,
                      hoeffding_radius, mean_dist_two_point, lipschitz_on, rho_legal, b_legal, b_conservative,
                      rlc_legal, twin_legal, avail_ok, legal_side_interval)
EPS = F(1, 100)
LAWS = {'mB': ('lin', F(-1, 2), ('x',)), 'mR': ('lin', F(1, 4), ('y',)), 'm0': ('const', F(1, 4))}
NX = ('y',)
HI = {'x': F(2), 'y': F(2), 'z': F(2)}

def fmt(q):
    return str(q) if not isinstance(q, F) else (str(q.numerator) if q.denominator == 1 else f'{q.numerator}/{q.denominator}')

def perturbed(s, i, dprime, eta):
    s2 = dict(s)
    s2[i] = s[i] + dprime
    for j, e in eta.items():
        s2[j] = s[j] + e
    return s2

def in_box(s, hi):
    return all(F(0) <= s[p] <= hi[p] for p in s)

def grid(a, b, k=5):
    return [a + (b - a) * F(t, k - 1) for t in range(k)]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S0 合法历史与可用性
print('=' * 78)
print('S0  T、形成记录与可用性（模型内由合法历史产生；读回物质宿载时为条件申报）')
print('=' * 78)
s_prev = {'x': F(0), 'y': F(1, 2), 'z': F(1, 2)}
pulse = F(1)                                  # 拍 t-1：界外入口脉冲写入 x；该拍无机制运行
S = dict(s_prev)
S['x'] = s_prev['x'] + (pulse if pulse > EPS else F(0))      # 数理式 4.2：有效脉冲入账
form_x = pulse > EPS and s_prev['x'] <= EPS                  # 第三篇 4.6（6）：有效脉冲使该位越阈
print(f'  拍 t-1 态 {{x: 0, y: 1/2, z: 1/2}}，界外脉冲 +1 于 x（有效）⇒ 拍 t 态 {{x: {fmt(S["x"])}, y: {fmt(S["y"])}, z: {fmt(S["z"])}}}，在盒 [0, 2]^3 内：{in_box(S, HI)}')
print(f'  T = {{x}}；Form(x) 在 t-1 有记录：{form_x}（REC 覆盖 t-1 至 t；记录起点 x = 0 ≤ eps，ENT 平凡）；Mark(x)：脉冲为界外信号')
tau_s = F(1, 40)
av = avail_ok(True, S['x'], tau_s, EPS, form_x)
print(f'  可用性原子：i ∈ T；阈上（状态读数 1 ± {fmt(tau_s)} 的下界 > eps）；Form 记录（另列，不由状态读数推出）⇒ Avail(x) = {av}')
for m, law in LAWS.items():
    c = value(law, S)
    print(f'  {m}: c = {fmt(c)}, hat c = {fmt(hat(c, EPS))}, Act = {int(hat(c, EPS) != 0)}, x ∈ Supp_act = {supp_act(law, S, "x")}, '
          f'P_HC(m; x) = {supp_act(law, S, "x") and av}')
print(f'  净增量（4.2）= {fmt(sum(hat(value(l, S), EPS) for l in LAWS.values()))}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S1
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S1  不完全干预 + 读数误差（有界与统计读法）；第三篇精确协议不可施行；合法域预算')
print('=' * 78)
gamma, beta, zeta, zeta_b, tau = F(1, 40), F(1, 10), F(1, 20), F(0), F(1, 40)
delta = F(-3, 4)
L_decl = F(1, 2)
n_units, alpha, pairs = 3, F(1, 20), 2
rho = rho_legal(S, NX, beta, HI)
b = b_legal(L_decl, rho, zeta_b)
print(f'  申报：gamma = {fmt(gamma)}, beta = {fmt(beta)}、N_x = {{y}}, zeta = {fmt(zeta)}, zeta_b = {fmt(zeta_b)}, tau = {fmt(tau)}, delta = {fmt(delta)}, L = {fmt(L_decl)}, 盒 hi = 2')
print(f'  合法副作用区间 y：{tuple(fmt(v) for v in legal_side_interval(S["y"], beta, HI["y"]))}；rho = {fmt(rho)}；b_legal = {fmt(b)}；保守预算 L·beta = {fmt(b_conservative(L_decl, beta, zeta_b))}')
a_each = alpha / (pairs * 2)
t_h = hoeffding_radius(n_units, tau, a_each)
r_b = gamma + tau
r_stat = gamma + min(tau, t_h)
print(f'  Hoeffding 半径（n = {n_units}，每均值 a = {fmt(a_each)}）t = {float(t_h):.6f}；r = gamma + min(tau, t) = {fmt(r_stat)}')
s1 = perturbed(S, 'x', delta, {'y': beta})
y0R, y1R = hat(value(LAWS['mR'], S), EPS), hat(value(LAWS['mR'], s1), EPS)
print(f'  第三篇 4.6（2）直接套用（eta_y = {fmt(beta)}）：mR 读数 {fmt(y0R)} → {fmt(y1R)}，R_mR(x) = {ch3_R(y0R, y1R)}，误判（真值 0）；按第三篇纪律判 ⊥（同其 7.5 实例乙）')

def sweep(m, rule):
    hits = tot = 0
    margins = []
    for dp, ey, k0, k1, e0, e1 in product(grid(delta - zeta, delta + zeta), grid(*legal_side_interval(S['y'], beta, HI['y'])),
                                          grid(-gamma, gamma), grid(-gamma, gamma), (-tau, F(0), tau), (-tau, F(0), tau)):
        s1 = perturbed(S, 'x', dp, {'y': ey})
        assert in_box(s1, HI)
        y0 = hat(value(LAWS[m], S), EPS) - k0 + e0
        y1 = hat(value(LAWS[m], s1), EPS) - k1 + e1
        tot += 1
        hits += (rule(y0, y1) == 1)
        margins.append(dist_sets(consistent(y0, r_b, EPS), consistent(y1, r_b, EPS)) - b)
    return hits, tot, min(margins), max(margins)

for m in ('mB', 'mR'):
    h, tot, mn, mx = sweep(m, lambda y0, y1: rlc_legal(y0, y1, r_b, EPS, L_decl, rho, zeta_b, av))
    print(f'  RLC（合法预算，有界读法 r = {fmt(r_b)}）{m}：判 P_HC(m; x) = 1 {h}/{tot}；dist - b ∈ [{fmt(mn)}, {fmt(mx)}]；'
          f'真值 P_HC = {int(supp_act(LAWS[m], S, "x") and av)}')
print('  （支持核由 RLC 认证；可用性由 S0 的原子给出；二者合取为 P_HC。）')
dist_e = mean_dist_two_point(n_units, tau)
for m in ('mB', 'mR'):
    ps = []
    for dp, ey, k0, k1 in product(grid(delta - zeta, delta + zeta, 3), grid(*legal_side_interval(S['y'], beta, HI['y']), 3),
                                  grid(-gamma, gamma, 3), grid(-gamma, gamma, 3)):
        s1 = perturbed(S, 'x', dp, {'y': ey})
        mu0 = hat(value(LAWS[m], S), EPS) - k0
        mu1 = hat(value(LAWS[m], s1), EPS) - k1
        ps.append(sum(p0 * p1 for e0, p0 in dist_e.items() for e1, p1 in dist_e.items()
                      if rlc_legal(mu0 + e0, mu1 + e1, r_stat, EPS, L_decl, rho, zeta_b, av) == 1))
    print(f'  统计读法 {m}：P(判 1) ∈ [{fmt(min(ps))}, {fmt(max(ps))}]（H0 时须 ≤ alpha/pairs = {fmt(alpha / pairs)}）')
cB = hat(value(LAWS['mB'], S), EPS)
mnD = min(abs(cB - k + e) for k in (-gamma, gamma) for e in (-tau, tau))
print(f'  Act（命题 13-A）：mB 的 |D| 最小 = {fmt(mnD)} > gamma + tau = {fmt(gamma + tau)}：{mnD > gamma + tau}；'
      f'补偿 1/10 > eps 时第三篇规则对 hat c = 0 的过程 = {ch3_act(F(1, 10), EPS)}（误判），13-A = {act_rule(F(1, 10), F(1, 10), F(0))}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S1b 边界态
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S1b 副作用邻位靠近上界（hi_y = 3/4，s(y) = 1/2）：合法半径截断，保守预算与合法预算分离（精确读数）')
print('=' * 78)
HIb = dict(HI); HIb['y'] = F(3, 4)
c0 = value(LAWS['mB'], S); c1 = value(LAWS['mB'], perturbed(S, 'x', delta, {}))
for bs in (F(1, 10), F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1), F(2)):
    rb = rho_legal(S, NX, bs, HIb)
    print(f'  beta = {fmt(bs):>5}：合法区间 {tuple(fmt(v) for v in legal_side_interval(S["y"], bs, HIb["y"]))}，rho = {fmt(rb)}；'
          f'保守 RLC（b = L·beta = {fmt(L_decl * bs)}）= {rlc_rule(c0, c1, F(0), EPS, L_decl * bs)}；'
          f'合法 RLC（b = {fmt(b_legal(L_decl, rb, F(0)))}）= {rlc_legal(c0, c1, F(0), EPS, L_decl, rb, F(0))}；'
          f'twin_legal = {"无" if twin_legal(c0, c1, S, NX, bs, HIb, L_decl, F(0)) is None else "有"}')
print('  读法：beta ≥ 3/4 时保守规则判 ⊥，但合法域内 H0 最大响应 L·rho ≤ 1/4 < |Δ| = 3/8，合法规则可靠地判 1（邻位靠近上界的情形）。')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S2
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S2  未申报 L：合法非零副作用可实现（rho > 0）时不可保证判 1；rho = 0 时无需 L')
print('=' * 78)
D = c1 - c0
for bs in (F(1, 10), F(1, 100), F(1, 1000)):
    rb = rho_legal(S, NX, bs, HI)
    tw = twin_legal(c0, c1, S, NX, bs, HI, None, F(0))
    law, etas, om = tw
    sB = perturbed(S, 'x', delta, etas)
    ok = (in_box(sB, HI) and hat(value(law, S), EPS) == hat(c0, EPS) and hat(value(law, sB), EPS) == hat(c1, EPS)
          and not supp_act(law, S, 'x'))
    print(f"  beta = {fmt(bs):>7}：rho = {fmt(rb)}；合法孪生 m' = {fmt(law[3])} + {fmt(law[1])}·s(y)，eta_y = {fmt(etas['y'])}，盒内且读数逐值相同：{ok}")
S0y = dict(S); S0y['y'] = F(0); HI0 = dict(HI); HI0['y'] = F(0)
rb = rho_legal(S0y, NX, F(1, 10), HI0)
print(f'  变式：y 的合法域退化为 {{0}}（hi_y = 0）：rho = {fmt(rb)}，b_legal = {fmt(b_legal(None, rb, F(0)))}，'
      f'未申报 L 时 rlc_legal = {rlc_legal(c0, c1, F(0), EPS, None, rb, F(0))}（副作用只能为 0，LOC 实为排他）')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S3
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S3  精确读数 beta 扫描（盒 hi = 2）：判 1 边界与合法孪生边界重合')
print('=' * 78)
print(f'  mB：c0 = {fmt(c0)}, c1 = {fmt(c1)}, |Δ| = {fmt(abs(D))}')
print(f'  （甲）带截距、L = 1/2：理论边界 L·rho = |Δ|，rho = min(beta, 3/2) ⇒ beta = 3/4')
for bs in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4) - F(1, 1000), F(3, 4), F(1)):
    rb = rho_legal(S, NX, bs, HI)
    v = rlc_legal(c0, c1, F(0), EPS, L_decl, rb, F(0))
    tw = twin_legal(c0, c1, S, NX, bs, HI, L_decl, F(0))
    if tw is None:
        txt = '合法孪生不存在（由 13-B 的可靠性证明，合法 H0 给不出此读数对）'
    else:
        law, etas, om = tw
        sB = perturbed(S, 'x', delta, etas)
        txt = (f"合法孪生 m' = {fmt(law[3])} + {fmt(law[1])}·s(y)，eta_y = {fmt(etas['y'])}，盒内：{in_box(sB, HI)}，"
               f"读数相同：{hat(value(law, sB), EPS) == hat(c1, EPS) and hat(value(law, S), EPS) == hat(c0, EPS)}")
    print(f'    beta = {fmt(bs):>9}：rlc_legal = {v}；{txt}')
print('  （乙）无截距、未申报 L，RLC*：紧性只在双侧余量子域（s(j_min) ≥ beta 且 hi - s(j_min) ≥ beta）内签署')
for bs in (F(1, 4), F(3, 8) - F(1, 1000), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)):
    v = rlc_star(c0, c1, F(0), EPS, bs, None, S['y'])
    sub = S['y'] >= bs and HI['y'] - S['y'] >= bs
    kp = c0 / S['y']; eta = D / kp
    sB = perturbed(S, 'x', delta, {'y': eta})
    ok = abs(eta) <= bs and in_box(sB, HI) and hat(value(('lin', kp, ('y',)), sB), EPS) == hat(c1, EPS)
    print(f"    beta = {fmt(bs):>9}：RLC* = {v}；子域内：{sub}；" + (f"无截距合法孪生 m' = {fmt(kp)}·s(y)，eta_y = {fmt(eta)}" if (v is None and ok) else
          ('—' if v == 1 else '孪生不合法或越出子域')))
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S4 群体
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S4  群体读数：支持存在、支持比例（M1 > B0）、与可用比例相交')
print('=' * 78)
N = 4
popA = [F(-1, 2)] * N
popB = [F(-1), F(0), F(-1), F(0)]
popC = [F(-1, 4), F(-3, 4), F(-1, 2), F(-1, 2)]
same = all(sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), perturbed(S, 'x', dp, {})), EPS) for k in popA)
           == sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), perturbed(S, 'x', dp, {})), EPS) for k in popB)
           for dp in grid(F(-4, 5), F(-7, 10), 7) + [F(0)])
print(f'  群体 A（全体 k = -1/2）与 B（-1, 0, -1, 0）在各设定值上平均读数逐值相同：{same}；个体层支持 4/4 与 2/4 ⇒ 「每一个体」不可认证（Theta 含 0）')
b_pop = b; r_pop = gamma + tau
M1 = EPS + F(1) * (abs(delta) + zeta) + b_pop
print(f'  B0 = {fmt(pop_null_bound(EPS, b_pop))}，M1 = eps + K(|delta| + zeta) + b = {fmt(M1)}（K = 1），M1 > B0：{M1 > pop_null_bound(EPS, b_pop)}')
for name, pop in (('A', popA), ('B', popB), ('C', popC)):
    sA = perturbed(S, 'x', delta + zeta, {'y': beta})
    y0 = sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), S), EPS) for k in pop) / N + gamma - tau
    y1 = sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), sA), EPS) for k in pop) / N - gamma + tau
    se = pop_supp_exists(y0, y1, r_pop, EPS, b_pop)
    kind, f = pop_fraction(y0, y1, r_pop, EPS, b_pop, M1)
    print(f'  群体 {name}：平均读数 {fmt(y0)} → {fmt(y1)}；支持存在 = {se}；支持比例：{kind} {fmt(f) if kind == "bound" else f}'
          f'（真支持比例 {sum(supp_act(("lin", k, ("x",)), S, "x") for k in pop)}/{N}）')
f_low = pop_fraction(sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), S), EPS) for k in popB) / N + gamma - tau,
                     sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), perturbed(S, 'x', delta + zeta, {})), EPS) for k in popB) / N - gamma + tau,
                     r_pop, EPS, b_pop, M1)[1]
print('  与可用性相交（命题 13-E（五）：f_low + a_low > 1 ⇒ P_HC^∃ = 1）：')
print(f'    （i）模型内每一个体经 S0 的合法历史形成（a = 1，A 栏）：P_HC^∃ = {pop_hc_exists(f_low, F(1))}')
print(f'    （ii）只有群体平均状态读数 1 ± {fmt(tau_s)}、盒 hi_x = 2、申报 FORM_PROTO：a_low = {fmt(avail_fraction_lower(F(1), tau_s, EPS, F(2)))}，'
      f'P_HC^∃ = {pop_hc_exists(f_low, avail_fraction_lower(F(1), tau_s, EPS, F(2)))}（证据不足，⊥）')
print(f'    （iii）同上而 hi_x = 1（个体状态在上界）：a_low = {fmt(avail_fraction_lower(F(1), F(0), EPS, F(1)))}，P_HC^∃ = {pop_hc_exists(f_low, avail_fraction_lower(F(1), F(0), EPS, F(1)))}')
print(f'    （iv）无形成记录（记录起点已阈上且未申报 ENT）：a_low 未定，P_HC^∃ = {pop_hc_exists(f_low, None)}；支持存在仍 = 1')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S5
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S5  抽样单元：同一培养的细胞共用批次偏差（Act，H0 过程）')
print('=' * 78)
tau_b, tau_e, cells = F(1, 20), F(1, 20), 400
t_ill = hoeffding_radius(cells, tau_b + tau_e, alpha / 2)
q = F(0)
for bs in (-1, 1):
    for k in range(cells + 1):
        e = bs * tau_b + tau_e * F(2 * k - cells, cells)
        if act_rule(e, F(0), t_ill) != 1:
            q += F(1, 2) * F(comb(cells, k), 2 ** cells)
print(f'  误把 n = {cells} 个细胞当独立单元：t = {float(t_ill):.6f}；H0 下 P(判 1) = 1 - {float(q):.3e}')
print(f'  按培养计单元（n = 1，支撑界 r = {fmt(tau_b + tau_e)}）：P(判 1) = 0')
print()
print('运行结束。')
~~~

A.7　run\_checks.py　运行入口：F 类单拍判据的有限核验（5.1—5.5、5.8）

~~~python
"""《物质场具化判定式》附录程序：F 类单拍判据的有限核验（随机生成；全称结论以正文的证明为准）。
C5a、C5a′、C5b 为对照：其孪生构造只检非负、未检状态上界，用以显示合法域版本（C9）的必要；C9—C12 为冻结合法域上的核验。
C0 相容集 I(y; r, eps) 的成员核对
C1 命题 13-B（RLC，Lipschitz 预算，线性律与双线性律类）的可靠性：判 1 ⇒ i ∈ Supp_act
C2 命题 13-C（RLC*，基线校准，线性律类）的可靠性
C3 退化相容：gamma = beta = zeta = zeta_b = tau = 0 时，RLC、RLC* ≡ 第三篇 R_m(i)；命题 13-A ≡ 第三篇 4.6（1）
C4 引理 13-B1：朴素差值规则 |y1 - y0| > b + 2r 不可靠（H0 下判 1 的实例计数与一例）
C5 命题 13-D（不可认证界）：RLC* 判 ⊥ 的有效区情形都有逐值相同的 H0 孪生（精确读数，未申报 L）
C6 命题 13-E（一）：群体存在型证书的可靠性；B0 可达
C7 M2：线性律支持原子的迁移引理与三类反例
C8 Hoeffding 半径在两点误差下的精确覆盖
"""
import random
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))   # 使 python3 -I 亦可运行
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_core import (legal_side_interval, rho_legal, b_legal, b_conservative, rlc_legal, twin_legal, diff_rule,
                      avail_ok, pop_supp_exists, pop_fraction, avail_fraction_lower, pop_hc_exists, dist_sets,
                      value, hat, supp_act, consistent, gap, rlc_rule, rlc_star, rlc_star_margin, act_rule, ch3_act,
                      ch3_R, pop_exists, pop_null_bound, hoeffding_radius, mean_dist_two_point, lipschitz_on)
rng = random.Random(20261102)
POS = ['p0', 'p1', 'p2', 'p3', 'p4']

def rq(lo, hi, den=8):
    """[lo, hi] 中分母为 den 的随机有理数。"""
    a = int(lo * den)
    b = int(hi * den)
    return F(rng.randint(a, b), den)

def rand_state(hi):
    s = {}
    for p in POS:
        s[p] = F(0) if rng.random() < 0.2 else rq(F(0), hi)
    return s

def rand_law(K, rmax, allow_bil=True, avoid=None):
    """|k| <= K 的随机律；avoid 给定时生成不读 avoid 的律（H0 构造之一）。"""
    kinds = ['const', 'lin', 'lin', 'bil'] if allow_bil else ['const', 'lin', 'lin']
    kind = rng.choice(kinds)
    k = rq(-K, K)
    pool = [p for p in POS if p != avoid] if avoid else POS
    if kind == 'const':
        return ('const', k)
    b0 = rq(F(-1), F(1)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)   # 数理式 D12 的截距
    if kind == 'lin':
        n = rng.randint(1, rmax)
        return ('lin', k, tuple(rng.choice(pool) for _ in range(n)), b0)
    return ('bil', k, (rng.choice(POS if not avoid else POS), rng.choice(pool)), b0)

def fmt(q):
    return str(q) if q.denominator != 1 else str(q.numerator)

out = []

def say(x=''):
    print(x)
    out.append(x)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C0
bad = 0
cnt = 0
for _ in range(400):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    y = rq(F(-2), F(2))
    r = rq(F(0), F(1))
    I = consistent(y, r, eps)
    for t in range(-200, 201):
        c = F(t, 50)
        cnt += 1
        mem = (y - r <= hat(c, eps) <= y + r)
        inU = any(a <= c <= b for a, b in I)
        if mem and not inU:
            bad += 1
        if inU and not mem and c not in (eps, -eps):
            bad += 1
say(f'C0 相容集：{cnt} 个格点核对，违例 {bad}（闭包端点 ±eps 除外）')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C1
viol = 0
n_h0 = 0
n_alt = 0
cert_alt = 0
for _ in range(30000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    K, rmax, hi = F(1), 3, F(2)
    s = rand_state(hi)
    cand = [p for p in POS if s[p] > eps]
    if not cand:
        continue
    i = rng.choice(cand)
    Ni = tuple(p for p in POS if p != i and rng.random() < 0.5)
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    zeta = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    zeta_b = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    gamma = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    tau = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    law = rand_law(K, rmax)
    if law[0] == 'bil':
        # 双线性律的状态上界：hi 内
        pass
    # 申报 H0 预算（与真实律无关，只依申报类与界）
    if Ni:
        b = max(K * rmax * beta, K * (2 * hi * beta + beta * beta), K * hi * beta) + zeta_b
    else:
        b = zeta_b
    delta = rq(-s[i], hi - s[i])
    if delta == 0:
        continue
    lo_d, hi_d = max(-s[i], delta - zeta), min(hi - s[i], delta + zeta)
    dprime = rq(lo_d, hi_d, 64) if lo_d <= hi_d else delta
    if not (-s[i] <= dprime <= hi - s[i]):
        continue
    s1 = dict(s)
    s1[i] = s[i] + dprime
    ok = True
    for j in Ni:
        e = rq(-beta, beta, 64)
        if s[j] + e < 0 or s[j] + e > hi:
            e = F(0)
        s1[j] = s[j] + e
    omega = rq(-zeta_b, zeta_b, 64) if zeta_b else F(0)
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1) + omega
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    v = rlc_rule(y0, y1, gamma + tau, eps, b)
    truth = supp_act(law, s, i)
    if truth:
        n_alt += 1
        cert_alt += (v == 1)
    else:
        n_h0 += 1
        if v == 1:
            viol += 1
say(f'C1 RLC 可靠性：H0 情形 {n_h0}，判 1 违例 {viol}；支持情形 {n_alt}，其中判 1 {cert_alt}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C2
viol = 0
n_h0 = 0
n_alt = 0
cert_alt = 0
for _ in range(30000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    hi = F(2)
    s = rand_state(hi)
    cand = [p for p in POS if s[p] > eps]
    if not cand:
        continue
    i = rng.choice(cand)
    Ni = tuple(p for p in POS if p != i and rng.random() < 0.5)
    if not Ni:
        continue
    smin = min(s[j] for j in Ni)
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    zeta = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    zeta_b = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    gamma = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    tau = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    L = None if rng.random() < 0.5 else rq(F(0), F(3))
    k = rq(F(-1), F(1))
    n = rng.randint(1, 4)
    law = ('lin', k, tuple(rng.choice(POS) for _ in range(n)))
    if L is not None and lipschitz_on(law, s, Ni, F(1), hi) > L:
        continue  # 申报须属实
    if smin == 0 and L is None:
        continue
    delta = rq(-s[i], hi - s[i])
    if delta == 0:
        continue
    dprime = delta
    s1 = dict(s)
    s1[i] = s[i] + dprime
    for j in Ni:
        s1[j] = s[j] + rq(-min(beta, s[j]), beta, 64)
    omega = rq(-zeta_b, zeta_b, 64) if zeta_b else F(0)
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1) + omega
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    v = rlc_star(y0, y1, gamma + tau, eps, beta, L, smin, zeta_b)
    truth = supp_act(law, s, i)
    if truth:
        n_alt += 1
        cert_alt += (v == 1)
    else:
        n_h0 += 1
        viol += (v == 1)
say(f'C2 RLC* 可靠性（申报无截距线性律子类）：H0 情形 {n_h0}，判 1 违例 {viol}；支持情形 {n_alt}，其中判 1 {cert_alt}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C2'
viol = 0
n_h0 = 0
ex2 = None
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    s = {'i': rq(F(1, 4), F(2)), 'y': rq(F(1, 8), F(2))}
    beta = rq(F(1, 100), F(1, 2), 100)
    k = rq(F(-2), F(2), 16)
    b0 = rq(F(-2), F(2), 16)
    law = ('lin', k, ('y',), b0)              # H0：不读 i，带截距
    delta = -s['i'] / 2
    s1 = {'i': s['i'] + delta, 'y': s['y'] + beta}
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1)
    n_h0 += 1
    if rlc_star(hat(c0, eps), hat(c1, eps), F(0), eps, beta, None, s['y']) == 1:
        viol += 1
        if ex2 is None:
            ex2 = (law, s, beta, c0, c1)
say(f"C2' RLC* 若用于带截距的 F 律（D12）：{n_h0} 个 H0 实例中误判 1 {viol}（故 13-C 须申报无截距）")
if ex2:
    law, s_, beta, c0, c1 = ex2
    say(f"   一例：m = {fmt(law[3])} + {fmt(law[1])}·s(y)，s(y) = {fmt(s_['y'])}，beta = {fmt(beta)}，分项 {fmt(c0)} → {fmt(c1)}")
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C3
mism = 0
tot = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    hi = F(2)
    s = rand_state(hi)
    cand = [p for p in POS if s[p] > eps]
    if not cand:
        continue
    i = rng.choice(cand)
    law = rand_law(F(1), 3)
    delta = rq(-s[i], hi - s[i])
    if delta == 0:
        continue
    s1 = dict(s)
    s1[i] = s[i] + delta
    y0, y1 = hat(value(law, s), eps), hat(value(law, s1), eps)
    a = rlc_rule(y0, y1, F(0), eps, F(0))
    b_ = ch3_R(y0, y1)
    c = rlc_star(y0, y1, F(0), eps, F(0), None, F(1))
    d = act_rule(y0, F(0), F(0))
    e = ch3_act(y0, eps)
    tot += 1
    if not (a == b_ == c and d == e):
        mism += 1
say(f'C3 退化相容：{tot} 例，RLC / RLC* / 第三篇 R_m(i) 与 命题 13-A / 第三篇 4.6（1）不一致 {mism}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C4
naive_bad = 0
tot = 0
example = None
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(1, 100), F(1, 4), 100)
    beta = rq(F(1, 100), F(1, 2), 100)
    L = F(1, 2)
    b = L * beta
    # H0：m 读 y 不读 i；基线分项恰在阈下
    sy = rq(F(1, 2), F(2))
    k = (eps / sy) * rng.choice([F(1), F(99, 100), F(1, 2)])
    law = ('lin', k, ('y',))
    s = {'i': F(1), 'y': sy}
    s1 = {'i': F(1, 2), 'y': sy + beta}
    if lipschitz_on(law, s, ('y',), F(1), F(3)) > L:
        continue
    y0, y1 = hat(value(law, s), eps), hat(value(law, s1), eps)
    tot += 1
    naive = 1 if abs(y1 - y0) > b else None
    if naive == 1:
        naive_bad += 1
        if example is None:
            example = (eps, beta, k, sy, y0, y1, rlc_rule(y0, y1, F(0), eps, b))
say(f'C4 朴素差值规则（裕度 b + 2r；只击穿此一条，常数裕度 eps + b + 2r 可靠，见 C12）：{tot} 个 H0 实例中判 1（误判）{naive_bad}；RLC 在这些实例上判 1 0（由 C1 的证明）')
if example:
    eps, beta, k, sy, y0, y1, v = example
    say(f'   一例：eps = {fmt(eps)}，beta = {fmt(beta)}，m = {fmt(k)}·s(y)，s(y) = {fmt(sy)}，读数 {fmt(y0)} → {fmt(y1)}，'
        f'|Δ| = {fmt(abs(y1 - y0))} > b = {fmt(F(1, 2) * beta)}；RLC = {v}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C5a
def twin_lin_intercept(c0, c1, sj, L):
    """H0 孪生 m' = b' + k'·s(j)，|k'| = L，eta = (c1 - c0)/k'，取 k' 的符号使 eta ≥ 0（保证 s(j) + eta ≥ 0）。"""
    D = c1 - c0
    if D == 0:
        return ('lin', L, ('j',), c0 - L * sj), F(0)
    kp = L if D > 0 else -L
    eta = D / kp
    return ('lin', kp, ('j',), c0 - kp * sj), eta

n_bot = n_twin = n_one = n_boundary = 0
n_hi_viol = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    L = rq(F(1, 8), F(2))
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1))
    gamma = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    tau = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    r = gamma + tau
    b = L * beta
    si, sj = rq(F(1, 4), F(2)), rq(F(0), F(2))
    law = ('lin', rq(F(-1), F(1)), ('i',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    delta = rq(-si, F(2) - si)
    c0 = value(law, {'i': si})
    c1 = value(law, {'i': si + delta})
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    if rlc_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b) == 1:
        n_one += 1
        continue
    n_bot += 1
    # 在相容集上取 |c1' - c0'| ≤ b 的一对真成员（精确构造：最近点，必要时向区间内侧移动 h），构造孪生
    I0, I1 = consistent(y0, r, eps), consistent(y1, r, eps)
    mem = lambda c, y: y - r <= hat(c, eps) <= y + r
    found = False
    boundary = False
    for P in I0:
        for Q in I1:
            g = gap(P, Q)
            if g > b:
                continue
            if P[1] < Q[0]:
                a0, a1, d0, d1 = P[1], Q[0], -1, 1
            elif Q[1] < P[0]:
                a0, a1, d0, d1 = P[0], Q[1], 1, -1
            else:
                lo_, hi_ = max(P[0], Q[0]), min(P[1], Q[1])
                a0 = a1 = (lo_ + hi_) / 2
                d0 = d1 = 0
            h = (b - g) / 4
            h0 = min(h, (P[1] - P[0]) / 2)
            h1 = min(h, (Q[1] - Q[0]) / 2)
            hs0 = [F(0)] if h0 == 0 else [F(0), h0, -h0]
            hs1 = [F(0)] if h1 == 0 else [F(0), h1, -h1]
            cands = [(a0 + u, a1 + v) for u in hs0 for v in hs1
                     if P[0] <= a0 + u <= P[1] and Q[0] <= a1 + v <= Q[1]]
            for p0, p1 in cands:
                if mem(p0, y0) and mem(p1, y1) and abs(p1 - p0) <= b:
                    tw, eta = twin_lin_intercept(p0, p1, sj, L)
                    tc0 = value(tw, {'j': sj})
                    tc1 = value(tw, {'j': sj + eta})
                    if (abs(eta) <= beta and sj + eta >= 0 and mem(tc0, y0) and mem(tc1, y1)
                            and not supp_act(tw, {'i': si, 'j': sj}, 'i')
                            and lipschitz_on(tw, {'j': sj}, ('j',), F(1), F(9)) <= L):
                        found = True
                        if sj + eta > 2:
                            n_hi_viol += 1
            if not found and g == b:
                boundary = True
    n_boundary += (not found) and boundary
    n_twin += found
say(f'C5a 〔对照：未保持状态上界，合法域版本见 C9〕命题 13-D（RLC 在只用该过程读数对的判据中极大；带截距线性律，申报 L，含补偿与误差）：RLC 判 ⊥ {n_bot} 例，'
    f'精确构造出读数相容的 H0 孪生 {n_twin} 例；未构造者 {n_bot - n_twin} 例，其中 dist = b 且下确界只在开端点 ±eps 取得的边界情形 {n_boundary} 例；判 1 {n_one} 例')
say(f'    对照：上列孪生只检 s(j) + eta >= 0，未检状态上界；取 hi = 2 时越上界的孪生构造 {n_hi_viol} 处（合法域版本见 C9）')
# 未申报 L：任意 beta > 0 下孪生总存在（带截距）
n_all = n_ok = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    beta = rq(F(1, 1000), F(1, 10), 1000)
    si, sj = rq(F(1, 4), F(2)), rq(F(0), F(2))
    law = ('lin', rq(F(-4), F(4)), ('i',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    delta = rq(-si, F(2) - si)
    c0, c1 = value(law, {'i': si}), value(law, {'i': si + delta})
    n_all += 1
    D = c1 - c0
    kp = (abs(D) / beta) if D != 0 else F(1)
    kp = kp if D >= 0 else -kp
    eta = D / kp if D != 0 else F(0)
    tw = ('lin', kp, ('j',), c0 - kp * sj)
    n_ok += (abs(eta) <= beta and sj + eta >= 0 and hat(value(tw, {'j': sj}), eps) == hat(c0, eps)
             and hat(value(tw, {'j': sj + eta}), eps) == hat(c1, eps))
say(f'C5a′ 〔对照：未检状态上界；合法域上的结论见正文 13-D（一）与 run_minimal S2〕未申报 L（带截距线性律）：{n_all} 例任意实例与 beta ∈ [1/1000, 1/10]，逐值相同的 H0 孪生 {n_ok} 例')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C5b
n5b_hi = 0
n5b_out_sub = 0
n_bot = 0
n_twin = 0
n_one = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    hi = F(2)
    s = rand_state(hi)
    cand = [p for p in POS if s[p] > eps]
    if not cand:
        continue
    i = rng.choice(cand)
    Ni = tuple(p for p in POS if p != i and rng.random() < 0.6)
    if not Ni:
        continue
    smin = min(s[j] for j in Ni)
    if smin == 0:
        continue
    jmin = min(Ni, key=lambda j: s[j])
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1))
    k = rq(F(-1), F(1))
    law = ('lin', k, tuple(rng.choice(POS) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3))))
    delta = rq(-s[i], hi - s[i])
    s1 = dict(s)
    s1[i] = s[i] + delta
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1)
    if abs(c0) <= eps or abs(c1) <= eps:
        continue  # 只核有效区
    v = rlc_star(c0, c1, F(0), eps, beta, None, smin)
    if v == 1:
        n_one += 1
        continue
    n_bot += 1
    kp = c0 / s[jmin]
    eta = (c1 - c0) / kp
    twin = ('lin', kp, (jmin,))
    sB = dict(s)
    sB[i] = s[i] + delta
    sB[jmin] = s[jmin] + eta
    if (abs(eta) <= beta and s[jmin] + eta >= 0 and hat(value(twin, s), eps) == hat(c0, eps)
            and hat(value(twin, sB), eps) == hat(c1, eps) and not supp_act(twin, s, i)):
        n_twin += 1
        if s[jmin] + eta > hi:
            n5b_hi += 1
        if not (s[jmin] >= beta and hi - s[jmin] >= beta):
            n5b_out_sub += 1
say(f'C5b 〔对照〕命题 13-C 的配对孪生（无截距子类、精确读数、有效区、未申报 L）：RLC* 判 ⊥ {n_bot} 例，构造出逐值相同的 H0 孪生 {n_twin} 例；判 1 {n_one} 例')
say(f'    对照：其中越状态上界 hi = 2 的孪生 {n5b_hi} 例；落在双侧余量子域（s(j_min) >= beta 且 hi - s(j_min) >= beta）之外者 {n5b_out_sub} 例——13-C 的紧性只在该子域内成立')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C6

tot = 0

for _ in range(5000):

    beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    L = F(1)

    N = rng.randint(1, 6)
    ys0 = []
    ys1 = []
    for _n in range(N):
        sy = rq(F(0), F(2))
        k = rq(F(-1), F(1)) / 2
        law = ('lin', k, ('y',) * rng.randint(1, 2))
        if lipschitz_on(law, {'y': sy}, ('y',), F(1), F(3)) > L:

        s = {'i': F(1), 'y': sy}
        s1 = {'i': F(1, 2), 'y': sy + rq(-min(beta, sy), beta, 64)}
        ys0.append(hat(value(law, s), eps))
        ys1.append(hat(value(law, s1), eps))
    y0 = sum(ys0) / N
    y1 = sum(ys1) / N
    tot += 1
    if pop_exists(y0, y1, F(0), eps, b) == 1:
        viol += 1
~~~

~~~python
eps = F(1, 100)
# 邻位靠近上界：hi = 1，s(y) = 1/2，beta = 1，L = 1
s9 = {'x': F(1, 2), 'y': F(1, 2)}; hi9 = {'x': F(1), 'y': F(1)}
law = ('lin', F(3), ('x',), -F(13, 10))
c0, c1 = value(law, s9), value(law, {'x': F(3, 4), 'y': F(1, 2)})
rho = rho_legal(s9, ('y',), F(1), hi9)
old_tw_eta = (c1 - c0) / F(1)          # 不看合法域的构造：|k'| = L = 1，eta = Δ/k'
mx = F(0)
for n in range(1, 7):
    for kk in range(-12, 13):
        k = F(kk, 12 * n)
        if abs(k) * n > 1:
            continue
        for t in range(0, 11):
            lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(s9['y'], F(1), hi9['y'])
            eta = lo_ + (up_ - lo_) * F(t, 10)
            mx = max(mx, abs(k * n * eta))
say(f'C9a 邻位靠近上界：c0 = {fmt(c0)}，c1 = {fmt(c1)}，|Δ| = {fmt(c1 - c0)}；rho = {fmt(rho)}，保守预算 L·beta = 1，合法预算 b_legal = {fmt(b_legal(F(1), rho, F(0)))}；'
    f'保守预算 RLC = {_rlc(c0, c1, F(0), eps, F(1))}，合法预算 rlc_legal = {rlc_legal(c0, c1, F(0), eps, F(1), rho, F(0))}；'
    f'不看合法域的孪生 eta = {fmt(old_tw_eta)} 使 y = {fmt(s9["y"] + old_tw_eta)} > hi = 1（非法，拒绝）；twin_legal = {twin_legal(c0, c1, s9, ("y",), F(1), hi9, F(1), F(0))}；'
    f'枚举合法 H0（读 y、重数 ≤ 6、|k|·n ≤ 1）最大 |Δ| = {fmt(mx)}')

s9 = {'x': F(1, 2), 'y': F(1)}
law = ('lin', F(2), ('x',))
c0, c1 = value(law, s9), value(law, {'x': F(3, 4), 'y': F(1)})
mxc1 = None
for n in range(1, 7):
    k = c0 / (n * s9['y'])                       # 无截距、只读 y（位集只有 x、y），基线定 k·n
    lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(s9['y'], F(1), F(1))
    for t in range(0, 11):
~~~
