~~~python
        mxc1 = v if mxc1 is None or v > mxc1 else mxc1
say(f'C9b 邻位在上界：c0 = {fmt(c0)}，c1 = {fmt(c1)}；RLC*（未申报 L）= {rlc_star(c0, c1, F(0), eps, F(1), None, F(1))}；不看合法域的孪生 eta = 1/2 使 y = 3/2 > hi = 1（非法，拒绝）；'
    f'y 的上侧余量 hi - s(y) = 0 < beta = 1，不在 13-C 紧性子域内（缩域外）；枚举无截距合法 H0 的最大设定后分项 = {fmt(mxc1)} < c1 ⇒ 合法域上判 1 可靠（直接论证见正文 5.3）')
# 随机：合法域上的可靠性与极大性，含 N 为空、L = 0、beta = 0、边界状态
viol = 0; n_h0 = 0; n_bot = 0; n_twin = 0; n_bd = 0; n_one = 0; n_unb = 0
cases = {'N空': 0, 'L=0': 0, 'beta=0': 0, '边界态': 0}
cons_ok = 0; n_sh = 0; n_sh_ok = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    P = ['i', 'j1', 'j2', 'o']
    hi = {p: rq(F(1, 4), F(2)) for p in P}
    st = {}
    for p in P:
        u = rng.random()
        st[p] = F(0) if u < 0.2 else (hi[p] if u < 0.4 else rq(F(0), hi[p], 16))
    st['i'] = max(st['i'], eps + F(1, 16)) if eps + F(1, 16) <= hi['i'] else hi['i']
    if st['i'] <= eps:
        continue
    Ni = tuple(p for p in ('j1', 'j2') if rng.random() < 0.6)
    beta = F(0) if rng.random() < 0.1 else rq(F(0), F(1))
    L = F(0) if rng.random() < 0.1 else rq(F(0), F(2))
    zeta_b = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)) if rng.random() < 0.3 else F(0)
    gamma = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    tau = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    r = gamma + tau
    if not Ni: cases['N空'] += 1
    if L == 0: cases['L=0'] += 1
    if beta == 0: cases['beta=0'] += 1
    if any(st[j] in (0, hi[j]) for j in Ni): cases['边界态'] += 1
    rho = rho_legal(st, Ni, beta, hi)
    bl = b_legal(L, rho, zeta_b)
    bc = b_conservative(L, beta, zeta_b)
    cons_ok += (bl <= bc)
    # 真实律：H0 或支持；申报 L 须属实
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        reads_ = tuple(rng.choice(['j1', 'j2', 'o']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)))
    else:
        reads_ = tuple(['i'] + [rng.choice(['j1', 'j2', 'o']) for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 2))])
    nN = sum(1 for q in reads_ if q in Ni)
    kmax = (L / nN) if nN else F(2)
    k = rq(-kmax, kmax, 64) if kmax > 0 else F(0)
    law = ('lin', k, reads_, rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    lo_d, up_d = -st['i'], hi['i'] - st['i']
    dprime = rq(lo_d, up_d, 64)
    s1 = dict(st); s1['i'] = st['i'] + dprime
    for j in Ni:
        lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(st[j], beta, hi[j])
        s1[j] = st[j] + (lo_ + (up_ - lo_) * F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8))
    omega = rq(-zeta_b, zeta_b, 64) if zeta_b else F(0)
    c0, c1 = value(law, st), value(law, s1) + omega
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    v = rlc_legal(y0, y1, r, eps, L, rho, zeta_b)
    if not supp_act(law, st, 'i'):
        n_h0 += 1
        viol += (v == 1)
    if v == 1:
        n_one += 1
        continue
    n_bot += 1
    I0, I1 = consistent(y0, r, eps), consistent(y1, r, eps)
    mem = lambda c, y: y - r <= hat(c, eps) <= y + r
    found = False; boundary = False
    for Pp in I0:
        for Q in I1:
            g = gap(Pp, Q)
            if g > bl:
                continue
            if Pp[1] < Q[0]:
                a0, a1 = Pp[1], Q[0]
            elif Q[1] < Pp[0]:
                a0, a1 = Pp[0], Q[1]
            else:
                a0 = a1 = (max(Pp[0], Q[0]) + min(Pp[1], Q[1])) / 2
            h = (bl - g) / 4
            h0 = min(h, (Pp[1] - Pp[0]) / 2); h1 = min(h, (Q[1] - Q[0]) / 2)
            for u in ([F(0)] if h0 == 0 else [F(0), h0, -h0]):
                for w in ([F(0)] if h1 == 0 else [F(0), h1, -h1]):
                    p0, p1 = a0 + u, a1 + w
                    if not (Pp[0] <= p0 <= Pp[1] and Q[0] <= p1 <= Q[1]):
                        continue
                    if not (mem(p0, y0) and mem(p1, y1) and abs(p1 - p0) <= bl):
                        continue
                    tw = twin_legal(p0, p1, st, Ni, beta, hi, L, zeta_b)
                    if tw is None:
                        continue
                    law_t, etas, om = tw
                    sB = dict(st); sB['i'] = st['i'] + dprime
                    okb = True
                    for j, e in etas.items():
                        lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(st[j], beta, hi[j])
                        okb &= (lo_ <= e <= up_)
                        sB[j] = st[j] + e
                    okb &= all(F(0) <= sB[q] <= hi[q] for q in sB)
                    tc0 = value(law_t, st); tc1 = value(law_t, sB) + om
                    okL = law_t[0] == 'const' or abs(law_t[1]) * sum(1 for q in law_t[2] if q in Ni) <= L
                    if okb and okL and mem(tc0, y0) and mem(tc1, y1) and not supp_act(law_t, st, 'i'):
                        found = True
                        # 变式 SH：后继也受盒约束时，tgt 位（另设 tg，状态 hi/2）上加常数过程能否使两次后继都在盒内
                        n_sh += 1
                        n_sh_ok += (abs(tc1 - tc0) <= F(1))
            if not found and g == bl:
                boundary = True
    n_twin += found
    n_bd += (not found) and boundary
say(f'C9c 合法域随机核验（状态有 40% 取边界 0 或 hi；N 空 {cases["N空"]}、L = 0 {cases["L=0"]}、beta = 0 {cases["beta=0"]}、邻位处边界态 {cases["边界态"]} 例）：')
say(f'    b_legal ≤ L·beta + zeta_b 恒成立 {cons_ok}/{cons_ok}；可靠性：H0 {n_h0} 例，rlc_legal 判 1 违例 {viol}')
~~~

say(f'    极大性：rlc\_legal 判 ⊥ {n\_bot} 例，构造出合法（设定、副作用、L、盒内）且读数相容的 H0 孪生 {n\_twin} 例；未构造者 {n\_bot - n\_twin} 例，'

~~~text
    f'其中 dist = b_legal 且下确界只在开端点取得的边界情形 {n_bd} 例；判 1 {n_one} 例')
say(f'    变式 SH（后继亦受盒约束，tgt 位上界取 1、状态 1/2）：孪生 {n_sh} 例中可由常数过程使后继入盒者 {n_sh_ok} 例（缺省按第三篇 3.5 合法扰动只要求后继非负，恒可满足）')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C10 无形成记录
eps = F(1, 100)
law = ('lin', F(1, 2), ('x',))
y0, y1 = value(law, {'x': F(1)}), value(law, {'x': F(3, 2)})
se = pop_supp_exists(y0, y1, F(0), eps, F(0))
av = avail_ok(True, F(1), F(0), eps, False)
a_low = F(0)   # 无形成记录：可用比例下界为 0
~~~

say(f'C10a 阈上而无形成记录：读数 {fmt(y0)} → {fmt(y1)}；支持存在 SuppEx = {se}；个体可用性原子 = {av}（阈上而无 Form 记录）；'

~~~text
    f'P_HC^∃ = pop_hc_exists(f, 0) = {pop_hc_exists(F(1), a_low)}（⊥）——群体规则只签「支持存在」')
viol = 0; tot = 0; cert = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)); N = rng.randint(1, 8); K = F(1); beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 4)); L = F(1, 2)
    b = L * beta; delta = F(1, 2)
    M1 = eps + K * delta + b
    ys0 = []; ys1 = []; states = []; sup = []; formed = []
    for _n in range(N):
        si = rq(F(0), F(2))
        start_low = rng.random() < 0.7          # FORM_PROTO：记录起点 ≤ eps，阈上即有越阈形成记录
        frm = start_low and si > eps
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            lw = ('lin', rq(-K, K), ('i',), rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2)))
        else:
            lw = ('lin', rq(-L, L), ('y',), rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2)))
        sy = rq(F(0), F(2))
        s_ = {'i': si, 'y': sy}
        s1_ = {'i': si + delta, 'y': sy + rq(-min(beta, sy), beta, 64)}
        ys0.append(hat(value(lw, s_), eps)); ys1.append(hat(value(lw, s1_), eps))
        states.append(si); sup.append(supp_act(lw, s_, 'i')); formed.append(frm)
    if not all(formed[n] == (states[n] > eps) for n in range(N)):
        # 只在申报 FORM_PROTO 的群体上使用可用比例下界
        a_low = None
    else:
        a_low = avail_fraction_lower(sum(states) / N, F(0), eps, F(2))
    kind, f = pop_fraction(sum(ys0) / N, sum(ys1) / N, F(0), eps, b, M1)
    f_low = f if kind == 'bound' else F(0)
    v = pop_hc_exists(f_low, a_low)
    tot += 1
    if v == 1:
        cert += 1
        if not any(sup[n] and formed[n] for n in range(N)):
            viol += 1
say(f'C10b 支持与可用相交（命题 13-E（五））：{tot} 个群体，P_HC^∃ 判 1 {cert} 例，其中无一个体同时支持与可用的违例 {viol}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C11 比例下界前件
say(f'C11a M1 < B0：eps = 1、b = 1（B0 = 2）、M1 = 1，读数 2 → 5/2：pop_fraction = {pop_fraction(F(2), F(5, 2), F(0), F(1), F(1), F(1))}；'
    f'无前件的公式 (1/2 - 2)/(1 - 2) = 3/2 > 真比例 1/2，不可用')
say(f'C11b M1 = B0 = 2：pop_fraction = {pop_fraction(F(2), F(5, 2), F(0), F(1), F(1), F(2))}')
say(f'C11c 不相容读数（B0 = 3/20，M1 = 1/2，平均差 1）：pop_fraction = {pop_fraction(F(0), F(1), F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 20), F(1, 2))}')
viol = 0; inc = 0; nb = 0; tot = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)); N = rng.randint(1, 8); beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 4)); L = F(1, 2); b = L * beta
    K = F(1); delta = F(1, 2); M1 = eps + K * delta + b
    ys0 = []; ys1 = []; sup = []
    for _n in range(N):
        si = rq(eps + F(1, 8), F(2)); sy = rq(F(0), F(2))
        lw = ('lin', rq(-K, K), ('i',)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else ('lin', rq(-L, L), ('y',))
        ys0.append(hat(value(lw, {'i': si, 'y': sy}), eps))
        ys1.append(hat(value(lw, {'i': si + delta, 'y': sy + rq(-min(beta, sy), beta, 64)}), eps))
        sup.append(supp_act(lw, {'i': si, 'y': sy}, 'i'))
    kind, f = pop_fraction(sum(ys0) / N, sum(ys1) / N, F(0), eps, b, M1)
    tot += 1
    if kind == 'incompatible':
        inc += 1
    if kind == 'bound':
        nb += 1
        viol += (F(sum(sup), N) < f)
say(f'C11d 随机（模型内读数）：{tot} 个群体，给出非平凡下界 {nb} 例，下界超过真支持比例的违例 {viol}；误报不相容 {inc}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C12 常数裕度
viol = 0; dom_fail = 0; dom_b0 = 0; tot = 0; gain = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4)); beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2)); L = rq(F(0), F(1)); b = L * beta
    r = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    sy = rq(F(0), F(2)); si = rq(eps + F(1, 16), F(2))
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        lw = ('lin', rq(-L, L), ('y',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))      # H0
    else:
        lw = ('lin', rq(F(-2), F(2)), ('i',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    c0 = value(lw, {'i': si, 'y': sy})
    c1 = value(lw, {'i': si / 2, 'y': sy + rq(-min(beta, sy), beta, 64)})
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64); y1 = hat(c1, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    d = diff_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b); R = rlc_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b)
    tot += 1
    if not supp_act(lw, {'i': si, 'y': sy}, 'i') and d == 1:
        viol += 1
    if d == 1 and R != 1:
        if b > 0:
            dom_fail += 1
        else:
            dom_b0 += 1
    if R == 1 and d != 1:
        gain += 1
eps, b, r = F(1, 10), F(1, 20), F(1, 40)
y0 = F(1); y1 = y0 + b + 2 * r + eps / 2 + 2 * r
~~~

say(f'C12 常数裕度 eps + b + 2r：{tot} 例中 H0 判 1 违例 {viol}；b \> 0 时 diff\_rule 判 1 而 RLC 未判 1 {dom\_fail} 例（RLC 支配之）；b = 0 时 {dom\_b0} 例（都是相容集只在开端点 ±eps 相接的边界情形，见正文 5.2）；RLC 判 1 而常数裕度未判 1 {gain} 例；'  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;f'一例：eps = 1/10、b = 1/20、r = 1/40，读数 1 → {fmt(y1)}：diff\_rule = {diff\_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b)}，RLC = {rlc\_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b)}')  
say('核验结束。')  
A.8　run\_channel.py　运行入口：统计组合、合法域边界、群体、同拍门控、比较型观测族与读出的有限核验（5.1、5.3—5.6、5.8、5.9）

~~~python
"""《物质场具化判定式》附录程序：统计组合、合法域边界、群体、同拍门控、比较型观测族与读出的有限核验 D1—D7
（随机种子 20261104；全称结论以正文的证明为准）。D1e 含 xi = 0 精确分支；D2a 用 b_bil_avail（被测位可用域）；
D2b、D2b′、D2c 用真相容集与严格不等式的精确求解器（t13_exact），逐个见证以真实 hat 回代；D5a 分列「从窗入口删除」与「从起点删除」；
D5c 含饱和仍可认证的正对照；D4c 限只读 g 的信息域。
D1 统计与组合：复合原子的并合界（共用读数）、簇内相关的嵌套半径、共用通道联合相容集、自适应选择、复制品态差（REP-xi）
D2 合法域边界：双线性律合法域上确界的可靠性与极大性；I_full 结构族的可达判定（可靠性、对格点的完备性、相对 I_pair 的增益、旁证通道）；后继入盒变式
D3 群体回读：双线性群体的全称回读（均值判据）与支持比例；比例下界取整后的紧性；抽检可用比例（无放回抽样的精确覆盖）
D4 𝒦_g 同拍门控通道：门控输入证书、稳健越阈证书、只读发放时的不可认证孪生、Λ_g = 0 时与第十一篇精确判据的对应
D5 比较型观测族与迁移：谱系型去除、律改写、模型中介读数；M2 的 Act 与双线性支持迁移条件
D6 目标接口：EPSC 读数与过程分项（不可识别孪生、SAM 下的识别）；Lipschitz 律（MWC 型）的局部响应证书与符号定性前件
"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import random
from fractions import Fraction as F
from math import comb
from t13_core import (value, hat, supp_act, consistent, gap, dist_sets, rlc_rule, legal_side_interval, rho_legal,
                      b_legal, rlc_legal, hoeffding_radius, mean_dist_two_point, lipschitz_on, act_rule)
from t13_ext import (fm_feasible, nested_radius, joint_channel_rule, b_bil_legal, twin_bil, ifull_feasible,
                      sh_feasible, gate_input_cert, robust_crossing_cert, mediated_preimage, pieces_with_slope)
from t13_exact import (true_pieces, in_true, fm_strict, ifull_exact, verify_ifull, sh_exact, verify_sh, b_bil_avail)
rng = random.Random(20261104)

def rq(lo, hi, den=8):
    return F(rng.randint(int(lo * den), int(hi * den)), den)

def fmt(q):
    return str(q.numerator) if q.denominator == 1 else f'{q.numerator}/{q.denominator}'

def say(x=''):
    print(x)

def member_pair(y0, y1, r, eps, b):
    """在 I(y0)×I(y1) 中找 |c1 - c0| ≤ b 的一对真成员（最近点及向内移动），找不到返回 None。"""
    mem = lambda c, y: y - r <= hat(c, eps) <= y + r
    for P in consistent(y0, r, eps):
        for Q in consistent(y1, r, eps):
            g = gap(P, Q)
            if g > b:
                continue
            if P[1] < Q[0]:
                a0, a1 = P[1], Q[0]
            elif Q[1] < P[0]:
                a0, a1 = P[0], Q[1]
            else:
                a0 = a1 = (max(P[0], Q[0]) + min(P[1], Q[1])) / 2
            h = (b - g) / 4
            h0, h1 = min(h, (P[1] - P[0]) / 2), min(h, (Q[1] - Q[0]) / 2)
            for u in ([F(0)] if h0 == 0 else [F(0), h0, -h0]):
                for w in ([F(0)] if h1 == 0 else [F(0), h1, -h1]):
                    p0, p1 = a0 + u, a1 + w
                    if P[0] <= p0 <= P[1] and Q[0] <= p1 <= Q[1] and mem(p0, y0) and mem(p1, y1) and abs(p1 - p0) <= b:
                        return p0, p1
    return None

# ================================================================ D1
say('=' * 78)
say('D1  统计与组合：多拍原子组合、相关读数、自适应选择')
say('=' * 78)
# D1a 并合界：Act 与支持核两检验共用基线读数（相依），各取 alpha/2
alpha = F(1, 20)
n = 12
tau = F(1, 10)
dist_e = mean_dist_two_point(n, tau)
t_half = hoeffding_radius(n, tau, alpha / 4)        # 每均值 alpha/4（支持核检验用两个均值），Act 检验用基线均值
# 原假设实例：Act = 1（分项 1/2），支持核 = 0（读 y，副作用 0），b = 0
p_joint_false = F(0)
p_hc_false = F(0)
for e0, p0 in dist_e.items():
    for e1, p1 in dist_e.items():
        y0, y1 = F(1, 2) + e0, F(1, 2) + e1
        hc = rlc_rule(y0, y1, t_half, F(1, 100), F(0))
        act = act_rule(y0, F(0), t_half)
        if hc == 1 and act == 1:
            p_joint_false += p0 * p1
        if hc == 1:
            p_hc_false += p0 * p1
say(f'D1a 复合 HDyn 证书（Act 与支持核共用基线均值，n = {n}，误差 ±1/10 两点）：P(复合误判 1) = {fmt(p_joint_false)} ≤ P(支持核误判 1) = {fmt(p_hc_false)} ≤ alpha/2 = {fmt(alpha / 2)}')
# D1b 嵌套半径与误用
tau_b, tau_e = F(1, 20), F(1, 20)
for G, m in ((3, 100), (5, 20), (10, 10)):
    nn = G * m
    t_nest = nested_radius(G, nn, tau_b, tau_e, alpha)
    t_naive = hoeffding_radius(nn, tau_b + tau_e, alpha)
    # 精确分布：簇均值 (1/G)Σ ±tau_b，单元均值 (1/n)Σ ±tau_e
    pb = {tau_b * F(2 * k - G, G): F(comb(G, k), 2 ** G) for k in range(G + 1)}
    pe = {tau_e * F(2 * k - nn, nn): F(comb(nn, k), 2 ** nn) for k in range(nn + 1)}
    P_nest = sum(x * y for bb, x in pb.items() for ee, y in pe.items() if abs(bb + ee) >= t_nest)
    P_naive = sum(x * y for bb, x in pb.items() for ee, y in pe.items() if abs(bb + ee) >= t_naive)
    say(f'D1b G = {G} 簇 × {m} 单元：嵌套半径 {float(t_nest):.5f}，P(|误差| ≥ t) = {float(P_nest):.3e} ≤ alpha：{P_nest <= alpha}；'
        f'误把 {nn} 单元当独立：半径 {float(t_naive):.5f}，P = {float(P_naive):.4f}（> alpha：{P_naive > alpha}）')
# D1c 共用通道联合相容集
viol = gain = tot = 0
ex = None
for _ in range(4000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    L, beta = F(1, 2), rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    b = L * beta
    r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    tch = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    sy = rq(F(0), F(2))
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        law = ('lin', rq(-L, L), ('y',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
        h0 = True
    else:
        law = ('lin', rq(F(-1), F(1)), ('i',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
        h0 = False
    c0 = value(law, {'i': F(1), 'y': sy})
    c1 = value(law, {'i': F(1, 4), 'y': sy + rq(-min(beta, sy), beta, 64)})
    o = rq(-tch, tch, 64)
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + o + rq(-r, r, 64)
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) + o + rq(-r, r, 64)
    v, _ = joint_channel_rule(y0, y1, r, tch, eps, b)
    vs = rlc_rule(y0, y1, r + tch, eps, b)
    tot += 1
    if h0 and v == 1:
        viol += 1
    if v == 1 and vs != 1:
        gain += 1
        ex = ex or (y0, y1, r, tch, eps, b)
say(f'D1c 共用通道（偏差同值进入两读数）：{tot} 例，H0 判 1 违例 {viol}；联合相容集判 1 而分别按半径 r + tau_ch 判 ⊥ 的增益 {gain} 例'
    + (f'；一例：y = {fmt(ex[0])} → {fmt(ex[1])}，r = {fmt(ex[2])}，tau_ch = {fmt(ex[3])}，b = {fmt(ex[5])}' if ex else ''))
# D1d 自适应选择：单次检验以精确分位数校准，K 个候选取最大者不校正
K, n, tau = 5, 20, F(1, 10)
dist_e = mean_dist_two_point(n, tau)

def exact_thr(a):
    vals = sorted({abs(e) for e in dist_e}, reverse=True)
    thr = None
    for v in vals:
        if sum(p for e, p in dist_e.items() if abs(e) >= v) <= a:
            thr = v
        else:
            break
    return thr

t1 = exact_thr(alpha)
p1 = sum(p for e, p in dist_e.items() if abs(e) >= t1)
pK = 1 - (1 - p1) ** K
tK = exact_thr(alpha / K)
pKc = 1 - (1 - sum(p for e, p in dist_e.items() if abs(e) >= tK)) ** K
~~~

say(f'D1d 自适应选择（{K} 个独立原假设候选，各 n = {n}）：单检验精确阈值使 P = {float(p1):.4f} ≤ alpha；取最大者不校正 P(误判 1) = {float(pK):.4f} \> alpha：{pK \> alpha}；'

~~~text
    f'Bonferroni（alpha/{K}）后 P = {float(pKc):.4f} ≤ alpha：{pKc <= alpha}。有界读法的证书对任何选择规则都零误判（命题 D-4（三））')
# D1e 复制品态差 REP-xi：在复制品上读 Act，回读参照态（xi = 0 用 13-A 精确分支）
viol = tot = n_xi0 = eq_xi0 = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    xi = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    gamma, r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)), rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    k = rq(F(-1), F(1))
    reads_ = tuple(rng.choice(['p', 'q']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)))
    law = ('lin', k, reads_, rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    Lam = abs(k) * len(reads_)
    sref = {'p': rq(F(0), F(2)), 'q': rq(F(0), F(2))}
    su = {p: max(F(0), sref[p] + rq(-xi, xi, 64)) for p in sref}
    D = hat(value(law, su), eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    rule = (1 if abs(D) > gamma + r else None) if xi == 0 else (1 if abs(D) > eps + gamma + r + Lam * xi else None)
    tot += 1
    if rule == 1 and not abs(value(law, sref)) > eps:
        viol += 1
    if xi == 0:
        n_xi0 += 1
        eq_xi0 += (rule == act_rule(D, gamma, r))
say(f'D1e REP-xi（xi > 0 用保守充分条件 |D| > eps + gamma + r + Λ·xi；xi = 0 用 13-A）：{tot} 例，参照态 Act = 0 而判 1 的违例 {viol}；xi = 0 分支与 13-A 逐值一致 {eq_xi0}/{n_xi0}')
# ================================================================ D2
say()
say('=' * 78)
say('D2  合法域边界：双线性律、I_full、后继入盒')
say('=' * 78)
# D2a 双线性
viol = n_h0 = n_bot = n_tw = n_one = n_nomp = n_notw = 0
P = ['i', 'j1', 'j2', 'o']
for _ in range(6000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    hi = {p: rq(F(1, 2), F(2)) for p in P}
    s = {}
    for p in P:
        u = rng.random()
        s[p] = F(0) if u < 0.25 else (hi[p] if u < 0.4 else rq(F(0), hi[p], 16))
    if s['i'] <= eps:
        s['i'] = min(hi['i'], eps + F(1, 8))
        if s['i'] <= eps:
            continue
    Ni = tuple(p for p in ('j1', 'j2') if rng.random() < 0.7)
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1))
    Kc = rq(F(0), F(2))
    gamma, tau_ = (rq(F(0), F(1, 16)), rq(F(0), F(1, 16))) if rng.random() < 0.5 else (F(0), F(0))
    r = gamma + tau_
    delta = rq(-s['i'], hi['i'] - s['i'], 16)
    zeta = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    dlo, dhi = max(-s['i'], delta - zeta), min(hi['i'] - s['i'], delta + zeta)
    dprime_max = s['i'] + dhi
    bb = b_bil_avail(s, 'i', Ni, beta, hi, Kc, P, dprime_max, eps)
    assert bb is not None            # 生成器保证 s_i > eps（D-5 的定理域）
    # 真实律：双线性，随机读取对
    j1, j2 = rng.choice(P), rng.choice(P)
    law = ('bil', rq(-Kc, Kc, 64), (j1, j2), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    dp = rq(dlo, dhi, 64) if dlo <= dhi else delta
    s1 = dict(s); s1['i'] = s['i'] + dp
    for j in Ni:
        lo, up = legal_side_interval(s[j], beta, hi[j])
        s1[j] = s[j] + rng.choice([lo, up, lo + (up - lo) * F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)])
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1)
    y0, y1 = hat(c0, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64), hat(c1, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    v = rlc_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, bb)
    if not supp_act(law, s, 'i'):
        n_h0 += 1
        viol += (v == 1)
    if v == 1:
        n_one += 1
        continue
    n_bot += 1
    mp = member_pair(y0, y1, r, eps, bb)
    if mp is None:
        n_nomp += 1
        continue
    tw = twin_bil(mp[0], mp[1], s, 'i', Ni, beta, hi, Kc, P, dprime_max)
    if tw is None:
        n_notw += 1
        continue
    lawt, etas, si_set = tw
    sB = dict(s)
    sB['i'] = si_set if si_set is not None else s['i'] + (dlo if dlo <= dhi else delta)
    ok = True
    for j, e in etas.items():
        lo, up = legal_side_interval(s[j], beta, hi[j])
        ok &= (lo <= e <= up) and (j in Ni or e == 0)
        sB[j] = s[j] + e
    ok &= all(F(0) <= sB[p] <= hi[p] for p in P)
    ok &= (lawt[0] == 'const' or abs(lawt[1]) <= Kc)
    ok &= not supp_act(lawt, s, 'i')
    ok &= (y0 - r <= hat(value(lawt, s), eps) <= y0 + r) and (y1 - r <= hat(value(lawt, sB), eps) <= y1 + r)
    n_tw += ok
say(f'D2a 双线性（带截距，|k| ≤ K，合法域，被测位可用 s_i > eps）：H0 {n_h0} 例，以 b_bil_legal 为预算的 RLC 判 1 违例 {n_h0 and viol}；'
    f'RLC 判 ⊥ {n_bot} 例，构造出合法双线性孪生 {n_tw} 例（未找到真成员对 {n_nomp}，构造失败 {n_notw}）；判 1 {n_one} 例')
# D2b I_full 结构族（真相容集、严格不等式、逐个见证回代）
viol = tot = cert = gain = wit_ok = wit_tot = grid_miss = grid_tot = clos_cons = 0
for _ in range(1500):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    hi_y = rq(F(1, 2), F(2))
    s_y = rq(F(0), hi_y, 16)
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    Ey = legal_side_interval(s_y, beta, hi_y)
    L = F(0) if rng.random() < 0.1 else rq(F(0), F(2))
    r, rR = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)), (F(0) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(F(0), F(1, 8)))
    s_g = rq(F(0), F(1))
    R = (rq(F(-1), F(1)), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    cR0 = hat(R[0] + R[1] * s_y, eps)
    S_base = s_g + cR0
    h0 = rng.random() < 0.5
    if h0:
        law = ('lin', rq(-L, L, 64) if L > 0 else F(0), ('y',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    else:
        law = ('lin', rq(F(-1), F(1)), ('i', 'y') if rng.random() < 0.3 else ('i',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
        if 'y' in law[2] and abs(law[1]) > L:
            law = ('lin', law[1], ('i',), law[3])
    s = {'i': F(1), 'y': s_y}
    eta = rng.choice([Ey[0], Ey[1], F(0), Ey[0] + (Ey[1] - Ey[0]) * F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)])
    s1 = {'i': F(1, 2), 'y': s_y + eta}
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1)
    cR1 = hat(R[0] + R[1] * (s_y + eta), eps)
    if S_base + hat(c0, eps) < 0 or s_g + cR1 + hat(c1, eps) < 0:
        continue
    # 读数取在阈值附近的比例提高，以覆盖开端点
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + (rng.choice([-r, r]) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(-r, r, 64))
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) + (rng.choice([-r, r]) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(-r, r, 64))
    yR1 = cR1 + rq(-rR, rR, 64)
    args = (y0, y1, r, eps, L, s_y, Ey, R, yR1, rR, S_base, s_g)
    feas, w = ifull_exact(*args)
    feas_cl, _wc = ifull_feasible(*args)
    tot += 1
    if feas_cl and not feas:
        clos_cons += 1
    if h0 and not feas:
        viol += 1
    if not feas:
        cert += 1
        if rlc_legal(y0, y1, r, eps, L, rho_legal({'y': s_y}, ('y',), beta, {'y': hi_y}), F(0)) != 1:
            gain += 1
        grid_tot += 1
        found = False
        for te in range(9):
            e = Ey[0] + (Ey[1] - Ey[0]) * F(te, 8)
            cRe = hat(R[0] + R[1] * (s_y + e), eps)
            if not (yR1 - rR <= cRe <= yR1 + rR):
                continue
            for Pc0 in true_pieces(y0, r, eps):
                for Pc1 in true_pieces(y1, r, eps):
                    for t0 in range(1, 8):
                        for t1 in range(1, 8):
                            a = Pc0[0] + (Pc0[1] - Pc0[0]) * F(t0, 8)
                            bq = Pc1[0] + (Pc1[1] - Pc1[0]) * F(t1, 8)
                            okL = (abs(bq - a) <= L * abs(e)) if L is not None else (e != 0 or a == bq)
                            if okL and in_true(a, y0, r, eps) and in_true(bq, y1, r, eps) \
                                    and S_base + hat(a, eps) >= 0 and s_g + cRe + hat(bq, eps) >= 0:
                                found = True
        grid_miss += found
    else:
        wit_tot += 1
        wit_ok += verify_ifull(w, *args)
say(f'D2b I_full 结构族（精确求解器：真相容集、严格不等式）：{tot} 例；H0 而判定无孪生（误判 1）{viol}；判 1 {cert} 例，'
    f'其中 I_pair 合法预算判 ⊥ 的增益 {gain} 例；判 1 情形的格点完备性核对漏判 {grid_miss}/{grid_tot}；'
    f'判 ⊥ 情形见证以真实 hat 回代全部观测与后继约束成立 {wit_ok}/{wit_tot}；闭包保守算法称有孪生而精确判定无孪生 {clos_cons} 例')
s_y, Ey, eps = F(1, 2), legal_side_interval(F(1, 2), F(1, 10), F(2)), F(1, 100)
cB0, cB1 = F(-1, 2), F(-1, 8)
R = (F(0), F(1, 4))
feas, _w = ifull_exact(cB0, cB1, F(0), eps, None, s_y, Ey, R, R[1] * s_y, F(0), F(1) + R[1] * s_y, F(1))
feas2, w2 = ifull_exact(cB0, cB1, F(0), eps, None, s_y, Ey, R, R[1] * s_y, F(1, 20), F(1) + R[1] * s_y, F(1))
~~~

say(f'D2b′ 旁证通道（chi\_E：mR 律冻结已知、精确读出 1/8，未申报 L）：H0 孪生存在 = {feas} ⇒ 支持核判 1；'

~~~python
    f'mR 读数半径 1/20 时孪生存在 = {feas2}，见证回代成立 = {verify_ifull(w2, cB0, cB1, F(0), eps, None, s_y, Ey, R, R[1] * s_y, F(1, 20), F(1) + R[1] * s_y, F(1)) if feas2 else None}')
# D2c 后继入盒（I_pair，tgt ∉ N_i 子类；精确求解器）
def plain_exact(y0, y1, r, eps, b):
    for Pc0 in true_pieces(y0, r, eps):
        for Pc1 in true_pieces(y1, r, eps):
            cons = [({'c0': F(1)}, Pc0[1], Pc0[3]), ({'c0': F(-1)}, -Pc0[0], Pc0[2]), ({'c1': F(1)}, Pc1[1], Pc1[3]), ({'c1': F(-1)}, -Pc1[0], Pc1[2]),
                    ({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1)}, b, False), ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1)}, b, False)]
            if fm_strict(cons, ['c1', 'c0'])[0]:
                return True
    return False

viol = tot = binds = eqv_bad = eqv_tot = wit_ok = wit_tot = 0
for _ in range(4000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    b = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    r = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    H = rq(F(1, 8), F(2))
    dlo = rq(F(-1, 2), F(0)); dhi = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        dlo = dhi = F(0)
    c0 = rq(F(-1), F(1)); c1 = c0 + rq(-b, b, 64)
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + (rng.choice([-r, r]) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(-r, r, 64))
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) + (rng.choice([-r, r]) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(-r, r, 64))
    d = rq(dlo, dhi, 64) if dlo < dhi else dlo
    A_ok = abs(d + hat(c1, eps) - hat(c0, eps)) <= H
    feas, w = sh_exact(y0, y1, r, eps, b, dlo, dhi, H)
    tot += 1
    if feas:
        wit_tot += 1
        wit_ok += verify_sh(w, y0, y1, r, eps, b, dlo, dhi, H)
    if A_ok and not feas:
        viol += 1
    plain = plain_exact(y0, y1, r, eps, b)
    if plain and not feas:
        binds += 1
    if max(abs(dlo), abs(dhi)) + b + eps <= H:
        eqv_tot += 1
        eqv_bad += (plain != feas)
say(f'D2c 后继入盒（I_pair 加常数背景，tgt ∉ N_i；精确求解器）：{tot} 例；H0 实例被判无孪生 {viol}；见证回代成立 {wit_ok}/{wit_tot}；'
    f'SH 使孪生不存在而无约束时存在 {binds} 例；充分条件 |d| + b + eps ≤ H 下与无约束精确判定不一致 {eqv_bad}/{eqv_tot}')

viol = tot = cert = 0

    N = rng.randint(1, 8)
    hi = rq(F(1, 2), F(2))
    sj = [F(0) if rng.random() < 0.15 else rq(F(0), hi, 16) for _n in range(N)]
    r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    mbar = sum(sj) / N + rq(-r, r, 64)
    rule_all = (mbar - r) > (1 - F(1, N)) * hi
    tot += 1
    if rule_all:
        cert += 1
        viol += not all(x > 0 for x in sj)
N, hi = 5, F(1)
pop = [F(0)] + [hi] * (N - 1)
~~~

say(f'D3a 双线性群体全称回读（结构同质、0 ∉ Theta 时 Supp\_n ⇔ s\_n(j) \> 0；判据 m̄ - r \> (1 - 1/N)·hi）：{tot} 例判 1 {cert}，违例 {viol}；'

~~~text
    f'紧例：N = 5、hi = 1、一个体为 0 其余为 1，均值 {fmt(sum(pop) / N)} = (1 - 1/N)·hi，判 ⊥ 且确有个体不支持')
viol = tot = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    N = rng.randint(1, 8)
    hi = rq(F(1, 2), F(2))
    sj = [F(0) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(F(0), hi, 16) for _n in range(N)]
    r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    mbar = sum(sj) / N + rq(-r, r, 64)
    flow = max(F(0), (mbar - r) / hi)
    tot += 1
    viol += (F(sum(1 for x in sj if x > 0), N) < flow)
say(f'D3b 由状态均值得支持比例下界 (m̄ - r)/hi（结构同质）：{tot} 例，违例 {viol}')
bad = tot = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    N = rng.randint(1, 10)
    B0 = rq(F(0), F(1, 2)); M1 = B0 + rq(F(1, 16), F(2), 16)
    d = B0 + (M1 - B0) * F(rng.randint(1, 100), 100)
    fstar = (d - B0) / (M1 - B0)
    k = -((-fstar * N).numerator // (-fstar * N).denominator) if False else -(-(fstar * N).numerator // (fstar * N).denominator)
    k = max(1, min(N, k))
    Mp = (N * d - (N - k) * B0) / k          # 支持个体共同差值
    ok = (B0 <= Mp <= M1 or Mp <= M1) and ((k * Mp + (N - k) * B0) / N == d)
    if k > 1:
        ok &= ((k - 1) * M1 + (N - k + 1) * B0) / N < d
    tot += 1
    bad += not ok
say(f'D3c 比例下界取整 ⌈N·f*⌉/N 的紧性（在差值可取 [0, M1] 与 B0 的可构造族中）：{tot} 例构造出恰含该数支持个体、平均差恰为 d 的群体；'
    f'少一个支持个体则达不到 d；失败 {bad}')
# D3d 抽检可用比例：无放回抽样，Hoeffding（1963 定理 4）
worst = F(0)
for (Npop, A, nsamp) in ((40, 30, 10), (40, 20, 12), (100, 70, 20)):
    a_s = F(1, 20)
    t = hoeffding_radius(nsamp, F(1, 2), a_s)    # 指标 ∈ {0,1}：支撑宽 1，取 tau = 1/2
    p = F(0)
    for x in range(max(0, nsamp - (Npop - A)), min(A, nsamp) + 1):
        pr = F(comb(A, x) * comb(Npop - A, nsamp - x), comb(Npop, nsamp))
        if F(x, nsamp) - t > F(A, Npop):
            p += pr
    worst = max(worst, p)
    say(f'D3d 抽检可用比例：总体 {Npop}、可用 {A}、抽 {nsamp}，下界 â - t（t = {float(t):.4f}）超过真比例的精确概率 {float(p):.3e} ≤ 1/20：{p <= a_s}')
# ================================================================ D4
say()
say('=' * 78)
say('D4  𝒦_g 同拍门控通道')
say('=' * 78)
viol1 = viol2 = tot = cert2 = 0
twin_ok = twin_tot = one_sided = 0
corr_bad = corr_tot = 0
for _ in range(6000):
    nin = 4
    act = [rng.random() < 0.6 for _k in range(nin)]
    hi_w = F(2)
    w = [rq(F(0), hi_w, 16) for _k in range(nin)]
    lam, v, I, theta = rq(F(0), F(1)), rq(F(-1), F(2)), rq(F(-1), F(1)), rq(F(1, 2), F(3))
    T = [k for k in range(nin) if rng.random() < 0.4]
    Nn = [k for k in range(nin) if k not in T and rng.random() < 0.5]
    side_v = rng.random() < 0.5
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    rv = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)) if rng.random() < 0.7 else F(0)
    Xi = sum((max(abs(x) for x in legal_side_interval(w[k], beta, hi_w)) for k in Nn if act[k]), F(0)) + (lam * beta if side_v else 0)
    vt = lambda ww, vv: lam * vv + sum((ww[k] for k in range(nin) if act[k]), F(0)) + I
    # 设定 T（[0, hi]），副作用于 N（合法）与 v
    w1 = list(w)
    for k in T:
        w1[k] = rq(F(0), hi_w, 16)
    w1s = list(w1)
    for k in Nn:
        lo, up = legal_side_interval(w[k], beta, hi_w)
        w1s[k] = w[k] + rng.choice([lo, up, F(0)])
    v1 = v + (rng.choice([-beta, beta, F(0)]) if side_v else F(0))
    vt0, vt1 = vt(w, v), vt(w1s, v1)
    z0, z1 = vt0 + rq(-rv, rv, 64), vt1 + rq(-rv, rv, 64)
    h0 = not any(act[k] for k in T)
    tot += 1
    c1 = gate_input_cert(z0, z1, rv, Xi)
    if h0 and c1 == 1:
        viol1 += 1
    c2 = robust_crossing_cert(z0, z1, rv, Xi, theta)
    if c2 == 1:
        cert2 += 1
        # 只以 T 的设定（无副作用）是否改变门控
        g0 = vt(w, v) >= theta
        gT = vt(w1, v) >= theta
        viol2 += (g0 == gT)
    # 只读发放时的孪生：A 有翻转且 Xi > 0（某方向可实现）
    g0, g1 = vt0 >= theta, vt1 >= theta
    if g0 != g1 and Xi > 0:
        # 孪生 B：T 不被驱动（以注入补偿 vt0），基线 vt 置于阈附近，以合法副作用翻转
        need_up = (not g0) and g1
        # 选一个可在所需方向移动的读集结点
        cand = []
        for k in Nn:
            if act[k]:
                lo, up = legal_side_interval(w[k], beta, hi_w)
                if (up > 0 if need_up else lo < 0):
                    cand.append(up if need_up else lo)
        if side_v and lam * beta > 0:
            cand.append(lam * beta if need_up else -lam * beta)
        if not cand:
            one_sided += 1          # 所需方向上无非零合法副作用（单侧受限），孪生不适用
        if cand:
            twin_tot += 1
            step = max(cand, key=abs)
            vt0B = theta - step / 2 if need_up else theta - step / 2   # need_up: vt0B < theta ≤ vt0B + step
            vt1B = vt0B + step
            gB0, gB1 = vt0B >= theta, vt1B >= theta
            twin_ok += (gB0 == g0 and gB1 == g1)
    # Λ_g = 0 时与精确判据的对应
    if Xi == 0 and rv == 0:
        corr_tot += 1
        exact_dep = sum((w1[k] - w[k] for k in T if act[k]), F(0)) != 0
        corr_bad += ((c1 == 1) != exact_dep)
say(f'D4a 门控输入证书（命题 G-1）：{tot} 例，H0（T 不在 gr_j）判 1 违例 {viol1}')
say(f'D4b 稳健越阈证书（命题 G-2）：判 1 {cert2} 例，其中「只以 T 设定、无副作用」时门控未改变的违例 {viol2}')
say(f'D4c 只读发放的信息域 𝔇_g（冻结：可见标签序列、发放读数；不冻结：输入模式映射 pat、注入 I_j（从而完整输入 σ = (a, I)）、v_j）：所需方向上有非零合法副作用的翻转实例 {twin_tot} 个，构造出发放序列相同的 H0 孪生 {twin_ok} 个；所需方向单侧受限 {one_sided} 个（孪生不适用，命题 G-3 的前件）')
say(f'D4d Λ_g = 0（Xi = 0、rv = 0）时 G-1 与精确判定「联合设定改变 vt（gr_j 中 T 位改变量之和 ≠ 0）」不一致 {corr_bad}/{corr_tot}')
# ================================================================ D5
say()
say('=' * 78)
say('D5  比较型观测族：删除比较、律改写、模型中介读数；M2 迁移条件')
say('=' * 78)
bad_w = bad_h = tot = pre_only = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    Hn, Wn = rng.randint(0, 3), rng.randint(1, 4)       # Hn 拍窗前历史，Wn 拍窗内
    laws = {f'p{q}': ('lin', rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2)), tuple(rng.choice(['a', 'b']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 2))), rq(F(0), F(1, 2)))
            for q in range(3)}
    tg = {nm: rng.choice(['a', 'b']) for nm in laws}
    runs_m = {t: (rng.random() < 0.7) for t in range(Hn + Wn)}   # m 是否在该拍运行（字母门控）
    def run(skip_from):
        st = {'a': F(1), 'b': F(1)}
        eff_hist, eff_win = False, False
        for t in range(Hn + Wn):
            inc = {'a': F(0), 'b': F(0)}
            for nm, lw in laws.items():
                if nm == 'p0' and not runs_m[t]:
                    continue
                c = hat(value(lw, st), eps)
                if nm == 'p0':
                    if c != 0:
                        eff_hist = True
                        if t >= Hn:
                            eff_win = True
                    if skip_from is not None and t >= skip_from:
                        continue
                inc[tg[nm]] += c
            st = {p: st[p] + inc[p] for p in st}
        return st, eff_hist, eff_win
    sA, eh, ew = run(None)
    sW, _, _ = run(Hn)          # 从窗入口删除（共同前缀）
    sO, _, _ = run(0)           # 从起点删除（谱系型）
    tot += 1
    if not ew and sA != sW:
        bad_w += 1
    if not eh and sA != sO:
        bad_h += 1
    if (not ew) and sA != sO:
        pre_only += 1
say(f'D5a 删除比较（命题 C-1）：{tot} 个随机 F 运行。从窗入口删除：「窗内 m 从未有效而差异 ≠ 0」违例 {bad_w}；'
    f'从起点删除：「历史中 m 从未有效而差异 ≠ 0」违例 {bad_h}；从起点删除而窗内无有效作用、差异仍 ≠ 0 的实例 {pre_only} 个（结论只能是历史层的）')
eps = F(1, 10)
a0 = F(1); a_orig = a0 - F(1, 2); a_del = a0     # 拍 0：m = -1/2（窗前）；拍 1（W）：m 不运行
~~~

say(f'    窗前作用反例：拍 0 m = -1/2，W = {{拍 1}} 内 m 不运行；原实例窗末 a = {fmt(a\_orig)}，从起点删除 a = {fmt(a\_del)}，差 {fmt(a\_del - a\_orig)} ≠ 0，'

~~~python
for _t in range(3):

    traj.append(st['a'])
say(f'    拍级 Act 不被认证：m = -a/2，eps = 1/10，a 的轨迹 {[fmt(x) for x in traj]}，末拍分项 {fmt(value(m_law, {"a": traj[-1]}))} 无效，而删除后 a 恒为 1，差 {fmt(1 - traj[-1])}')
# D5b 律改写
viol = tot = 0
for _ in range(4000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4)); zb = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)); r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    s = {'R': rq(F(0), F(2)), 'u': rq(F(0), F(2))}
    r0 = rq(F(0), F(2))
    h0 = rng.random() < 0.5
    law = ('lin', rq(F(-1), F(1)), ('u',) if h0 else ('R', 'u'), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    c = value(law, s)
    cs = value(law, {'R': r0, 'u': s['u']}) + rq(-zb, zb, 64)
    y, ys = hat(c, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64), hat(cs, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    v = rlc_rule(y, ys, r, eps, zb)
    tot += 1
    if v == 1 and not (law[1] != 0 and 'R' in law[2] and r0 != s['R']):
        viol += 1
say(f'D5b 律改写（把过程对 R 的读取固定为 r0；旁路 ≤ zeta_b）：{tot} 例，「判依赖 R 而律不读 R 或系数为 0」的违例 {viol}')
# D5c 模型中介读数
phi = [(F(0), F(1), F(0), F(2)), (F(1), F(2), F(2), F(3)), (F(2), F(4), F(3), F(3))]   # 单调、在 [2, 4] 饱和

def phi_of(x):
    for x0, x1, y0, y1 in phi:
        if x0 <= x <= x1:
            return y0 + (y1 - y0) * (x - x0) / (x1 - x0)
viol = tot = 0
for _ in range(4000):
    tau, zp = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)), rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    th0 = rq(F(0), F(4), 16)
    th1 = th0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else rq(F(0), F(4), 16)
    z0 = phi_of(th0) + rq(-zp, zp, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    z1 = phi_of(th1) + rq(-zp, zp, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    pre0 = mediated_preimage(z0, tau, zp, phi)
    pre1 = mediated_preimage(z1, tau, zp, phi)
    inside = any(a <= th0 <= b_ for a, b_ in pre0) and any(a <= th1 <= b_ for a, b_ in pre1)
    certify = (pre0 and pre1 and dist_sets(pre0, pre1) > 0)
    tot += 1
    viol += (not inside) or (certify and th0 == th1)
say(f'D5c 模型中介读数（单调分段线性 phi，在 [2, 4] 饱和）：{tot} 例，真值不在原像相容集内或「判变化而真值未变」的违例 {viol}')
phi2 = [(F(0), F(1), F(0), F(1)), (F(1), F(2), F(1), F(1))]
A_ = mediated_preimage(F(1), F(0), F(0), phi2); B_ = mediated_preimage(F(0), F(0), F(0), phi2)
~~~

~~~text
# D5d M2 迁移
viol = tot = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    tb, tk, ts = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)), rq(F(0), F(1, 8)), rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    hi = F(2)
    reads_ = tuple(rng.choice(['p', 'q']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 2)))
    k, b0 = rq(F(-1), F(1)), rq(F(-1), F(1))
    ks, bs = k + rq(-tk, tk, 64), b0 + rq(-tb, tb, 64)
    s = {'p': rq(F(0), hi), 'q': rq(F(0), hi)}
    sst = {p: min(hi, max(F(0), s[p] + rq(-ts, ts, 64))) for p in s}
    gamma, r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)), rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    c = value(('lin', k, reads_, b0), s)
    D = hat(c, eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    c_low = abs(D) - gamma - r
    Dtr = tb + tk * len(reads_) * hi + abs(k) * len(reads_) * ts
    rule = 1 if (hat(c, eps) != 0 or abs(D) > gamma + r) and c_low - Dtr > eps and abs(D) > gamma + r else None
    tot += 1
    if rule == 1 and not abs(value(('lin', ks, reads_, bs), sst)) > eps:
        viol += 1
say(f'D5d M2 的 Act 迁移条件（律容差 tb、tk，态容差 ts，迁移余量 Δ_tr = tb + tk·n·hi + |k|·n·ts）：{tot} 例，目标处 Act = 0 而判迁移的违例 {viol}')
bad = tot = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    tk = rq(F(0), F(1, 4)); k = rq(F(-1), F(1)); ks = k + rq(-tk, tk, 64)
    sj2 = rq(F(0), F(1)); rs = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)); read = sj2 + rq(-rs, rs, 64)
    rule = abs(k) > tk and read - rs > 0

    if rule and not supp_act(('bil', ks, ('i', 'j')), {'i': F(1), 'j': sj2}, 'i'):

dep2 = [F(0), F(0), F(0), F(0)]; rel2 = [F(-1, 8), F(-1, 4), F(-1, 8), F(0)]   # 实例二：压抑过程无作用，弛豫给出同样总量
~~~

~~~text
    f'实例一压抑过程分项和 {fmt(sum(dep1[i] for i in A_set))}，实例二为 0 ⇒ 总响应变化不识别压抑过程的分项（命题 I-1）')
say(f'    SAM_W′（窗内只有压抑过程作用于 A 中各位）时 z 差 = 压抑分项和：{z(post1) - z(w_pre) == sum(dep1[i] for i in A_set)}；'

# D6b Lipschitz 律（MWC 型有理函数）
viol = tot = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    V, Km = rq(F(1, 4), F(2)), rq(F(1, 4), F(2))
    hi = F(2)
    sy = rq(F(0), hi, 16)
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    Ey = legal_side_interval(sy, beta, hi)
    Lip = V / Km                     # sup |d/dy V y/(Km + y)| on [0, hi]
    b = Lip * max(Ey[1], -Ey[0])
    h0 = rng.random() < 0.5
    si = F(1)
    def c_of(si_, sy_):
        base = V * sy_ / (Km + sy_) + F(1, 10)
        return base if h0 else base + V * si_ / (Km + si_)
    eta = rng.choice([Ey[0], Ey[1], F(0)])
    c0, c1 = c_of(si, sy), c_of(F(1, 4), sy + eta)
    r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    y0, y1 = hat(c0, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64), hat(c1, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    v = rlc_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b)
    tot += 1
    viol += (h0 and v == 1)
say(f'D6b Lipschitz 律 c = V·y/(Km + y) + 1/10（H0：与 s_i 无关），预算 (V/Km)·rho：{tot} 例，H0 判 1 违例 {viol}')
~~~

~~~text
# 阈下读数：保证判 1 的闭式只在全部有效单段区前件下适用
~~~

A.9　run\_multitick.py　运行入口：扩展类闭合、M-1、M-2 与 F-1 的有限核验（3.6、5.7、8.1）

~~~python
"""《物质场具化判定式》附录程序：多拍门控通道的有限核验 N0—N6（随机种子 20261106；全称结论以正文的证明为准）。
N0 四个边界见证：缺 plastic 开关的递推、同拍互相输入、反复使用同一标签的孪生、RESET 分项型
N1 命题 M-1 的可靠性：判 1 ⇒ 只以 T 设定（无副作用）的运行确实改变 e；四类（𝒦_g、第三因子、𝒦_PA^δ、𝒦_PA^≺）×
   plastic/bap 四种组合 × ρ ∈ {0, 1, 其余}，全部事件种类；副作用造成的假改变全部拒绝；引理 R1′ 逐拍核对
N2 传播界本身：裕度成立时，两运行逐拍逐结点之差 ≤ D(t)，各事件分项差 ≤ B_e；开关系数的定向表（plastic/bap × ρ = 0、1）
N3 零通道对应：Λ = 0 时 M-1 判 1 ⟺ 实现的 σ′ 改变 e，覆盖定义 5 全部种类（含 RESET 分项型）；不与定理 D1 等价
N4 扩展类的闭合：𝒦_PA^≺ 每拍同时方程恰一解且与次序计算一致、与拓扑次序的选取无关；同拍循环出现无解或多解
N5 命题 F-1（𝒦_PA^δ 主版本与 𝒦_PA^≺ 版本）：FD 见证、回返分量的定量预测、阻断回返时 T 不改变 DAN 及其门控事件、干预界 κ*
N6 命题 M-2：全局固定 pat_B、反复使用同一标签、两方向单次翻转；逐模型核查 pat_B 一致性与 [0, t_e] 完整可见记录
"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import random
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_gate import (run, step, validate, r1_check, simultaneous_solutions, prop_bounds, prop_bounds_no_switch,
                      event_bound, m1_certificate, beta_star, changed, m2_twin, copy_state)
rng = random.Random(20261106)
ZERO = F(0)

def rq(lo, hi, den=8):
    return F(rng.randint(int(lo * den), int(hi * den)), den)

def say(x=''):
    print(x)

def zeros_D(nin, nout):
    return ([[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nin)], [ZERO] * nout, [ZERO] * nin, [ZERO] * nout, [0] * nout)

# ───────────────────────── N0 边界见证 ─────────────────────────
say('N0 边界见证')
# 缺开关递推的对照：两输入一输出，T = {w00}，非 T 位 w10 = 1/2，λ = 0，θ = 3/5，ρ = 1，plastic = False，A± = 0
P = {'lam': [F(0)], 'theta': [F(3, 5)], 'vres': [F(0)], 'dx': F(1), 'dy': F(1), 'Ap': F(0), 'Am': F(0), 'rho': F(1),
     'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': False, 'bap': True, 'b': [[F(0)], [F(0)]], 'gate_of': [0], 'mode': 'g'}
st = ([[F(1)], [F(1, 2)]], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [0])
seq = [([0, 0], [F(0)]), ([0, 1], [F(0)])]
act = run(P, st, seq)
obs = run(P, st, seq, 0, {(0, 0): F(3, 2)}, {('w', 1, 0): F(1, 5)})
pure = run(P, st, seq, 0, {(0, 0): F(3, 2)}, {})
z = {t: obs[t]['vt'] for t in range(2)}
g = {t: obs[t]['g'] for t in range(2)}
D0 = ([[F(0)], [F(1, 5)]], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [0])
e = ('YIN', 0)
new = m1_certificate(P, seq, 0, 1, z, ZERO, g, D0, e, obs[1]['ev'][e], act[1]['ev'][e])
old = m1_certificate(P, seq, 0, 1, z, ZERO, g, D0, e, obs[1]['ev'][e], act[1]['ev'][e], bounds=prop_bounds_no_switch)
~~~

say(f'  缺开关递推的对照：设定实验 vt(1) = {obs\[1\]\["vt"\]\[0\]}、发放 {obs\[1\]\["g"\]\[0\]}；只设 T 时发放 {pure\[1\]\["g"\]\[0\]}（改变全由副作用）；'

~~~text
    f'含开关的递推判词 {new[0]}（拒绝），缺开关的递推判词 {old[0]}（误判 1）')
assert new[0] is None and old[0] == 1 and obs[1]['g'][0] and not pure[1]['g'][0]
Dvt, Dst = prop_bounds(P, seq, 0, 1, g, D0)
say(f'    含开关递推 D_w10 拍 0 → 拍 1：{Dst[0][0][1][0]} → {Dst[1][0][1][0]}（plastic 关，系数 1）；拍 1 裕度 7/10 − {Dvt[1][0]} < 3/5')
# 同拍互相输入
Pm = {'lam': [F(0), F(0)], 'theta': [F(1, 2), F(1, 2)], 'vres': [F(0)] * 2, 'dx': F(0), 'dy': F(0), 'Ap': F(0), 'Am': F(0),
      'rho': F(0), 'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': False, 'bap': False, 'b': [[F(0)] * 2], 'gate_of': [0, 1],
      'U': [[F(0), F(1)], [F(1), F(0)]]}
st2 = ([[F(0), F(0)]], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [0, 0])
sols = simultaneous_solutions(Pm, st2, [0], [F(0), F(0)])
ok_dag = validate({**Pm, 'mode': 'dag', 'order': [0, 1]}, 1, 2)
Pmd = {**Pm, 'mode': 'delay'}
r_a = run(Pmd, st2, [([0], [F(0), F(0)])] * 3)
st2b = ([[F(0), F(0)]], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [1, 1])
r_b = run(Pmd, st2b, [([0], [F(0), F(0)])] * 3)
~~~

say(f'  同拍互相输入：同时方程 vt1 = g2、vt2 = g1、θ = 1/2 的解 {sols}（不唯一，不属本篇工作域）；𝒦\_PA^≺ 类检查 {ok\_dag}；'

~~~text
assert sols == [(0, 0), (1, 1)] and not ok_dag[0]
~~~
